## FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

### DELIVERABLE
Single 4:5 vertical advertising image. Format: JPG/PNG. Resolution: 1080 × 1350px minimum (Instagram/mobile-first density). Colour-corrected for iOS display, test against warm and daylight monitors.

---

### OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
Produce a premium beverage hero-shot that positions mosambi sparkling water as a contemporary, sophisticated everyday choice for urban adults 22–35. The product must be unmistakably the focal point. Visual strategy should signal "refreshment + refinement + contemporary ease" without deploying recognisable Indian visual clichés (no textiles, mandalas, spice market, gold foil, heritage fonts, temple elements, or saturated colour symbolism). The image must feel like it belongs in a modern urban lifestyle context—minimalist, clean, aspirational—while the mosambi ingredient itself guarantees cultural grounding.

---

### CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
**Product-as-gateway.** The bottle is the only storyteller. It contains real, recognisable mosambi (visible in the liquid and optionally in a single fresh segment) combined with visible carbonation. The surrounding environment is geometrically composed, neutral in palette, with deliberately negative space to enforce premium positioning. The image reads: "Familiar fruit, unfamiliar sophistication." No narrative, no face, no lifestyle staging—just the object itself and the space it commands.

---

### MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. **Primary:** The product bottle (brand, volume, liquid colour and carbonation)
2. **Secondary:** The presence of real mosambi (visual cue: juice colour, possibly a thin slice or segment as garnish/proof)
3. **Tertiary:** The impression of cleanliness, modernity, and refinement (lighting, space, finish)

No text is necessary. The product *is* the message.

---

### VISUAL_SYSTEM

**Framing and Composition:**
- **Vertical axis:** Product bottle positioned in the upper-middle third, leaving strong negative space below (per Samara: negative space is a shape of equal importance; use this to enforce perceived quality and breathing room)
- **Lighting:** Single, clean light source creating one soft shadow. Avoid harsh multiple shadows. The goal is clarity and polish, not drama
- **Colour palette:** Cool-to-neutral base (soft white, pale grey, or very soft concrete/stone texture). The mosambi inside the bottle should be the only saturated colour element—warm golden-orange juice with visible carbonation bubbles catching light
- **Foreground/support:** A simple prop (minimal—either a thin glass, a single mosambi segment on a neutral surface, or nothing) positioned lower to create visual weight distribution without distraction
- **Depth:** Shallow depth of field acceptable; bottle in sharp focus, background subtly blurred to reinforce that the product is the hero
- **Material language:** Glass clarity, condensation on the bottle (conveys freshness and chill), clean industrial or Scandinavian aesthetic

**Cultural positioning:** The aesthetic is *contemporary minimalism*, not Indian. The mosambi origin is signalled by the fruit itself (unmistakable to Indian consumers, discovered by others), not by typography or décor.

---

### PRODUCTION_RECIPE

1. **Setup:**
   - Simple white or soft grey cyclorama backdrop, or a subtle stone/concrete textured surface (minimal pattern, high contrast control)
   - Single key light at 45° or slightly higher, producing one defined shadow
   - Reflector or fill card opposite the key to control shadow depth (not eliminate it)
   - No background clutter; negative space is intentional

2. **Bottle and content:**
   - Use the actual product bottle if available; if not, a clear glass bottle with custom or near-final label
   - Fill with fresh mosambi juice (golden-orange, vitamin C-rich colour) or a juice-water blend that replicates the final colour
   - Carbonate the liquid in-camera or composite captured bubbles; bubbles should catch and scatter light
   - Optionally include one fresh mosambi segment (thinly sliced) on the surface below, or inside the glass if it's a serving shot
   - Bottle should show condensation or a light mist (spray with distilled water if needed; will evaporate, so plan timing)

3. **Camera and technical:**
   - Shoot at f/2.8–f/5.6 (shallow to moderate depth of field, bottle sharp)
   - Colour temperature: ~5500K (daylight) to lean slightly cool and fresh
   - Avoid high ISO; prioritise lighting over speed
   - Shoot in RAW for post-processing latitude

4. **Post-processing:**
   - Colour: Boost clarity on the mosambi juice and carbonation; keep background soft and neutral
   - Contrast: Lift blacks slightly (premium, not dark and heavy); ensure the bottle silhouette is clean against background
   - Vibrance: Lift the mosambi colour subtly without oversaturating (should feel natural, not candy)
   - Sharpening: Bottle and liquid only; background diffused
   - No vignetting; clean white or grey edges

---

### GENERATION_PROMPTS

**Single final generation prompt (for image synthesis if applicable):**

> A 4:5 vertical product photography shot of a premium sparkling mosambi beverage bottle, centred in the upper-middle frame against a soft white or pale grey background with generous negative space below. The bottle is clear glass, showing vivid golden-orange fresh mosambi juice inside with visible fine carbonation bubbles catching clean directional light from the left. A single fresh mosambi fruit segment rests on a neutral grey surface just below the bottle, slightly to the right. Lighting is soft and singular—one key light at 45°, creating one subtle shadow to the right. The aesthetic is contemporary minimalist, Scandinavian-influenced, clean and refined. High contrast, sharp bottle, soft diffused background. No text, no people, no cultural decorative elements. Photograph, professional studio product shot, high resolution, no grain. Beverage photography, premium brand positioning.

---

### DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS

- **Bottle label:** Deterministic. Use the final approved product label (or a near-final mockup). Do not synthesise the label; composite it in post or use the physical bottle.
- **Mosambi segment (if included):** Fresh fruit, real. Photograph it separately with consistent lighting and composite, or include on set. Colour and texture must be authentic.
- **Liquid colour:** Should match the actual mosambi juice colour (warm golden-orange). If generated, this must be colour-corrected to match reference samples of real mosambi juice.
- **Background surface:** Deterministic. Shoot on actual material (white card, paint, or stone texture) for consistent texture and reflectivity. Do not synthesise.

---

### AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable for a static image. No audio or animation required.

---

### FAILURE_PREVENTION

**Risk:** The generated image looks like a generic soft-drink advertisement.  
**Mitigation:** Enforce negative space, minimal propping, and cool/neutral palette. Avoid warm backgrounds, tropical elements, or abundance of fruit. The simplicity *is* the premium signal.

**Risk:** The mosambi juice colour appears artificial or too saturated.  
**Mitigation:** Reference real mosambi juice colour cards before generation/post-processing. Mosambi is softer and less acidic-looking than lime or lemon; err toward golden-orange rather than neon.

**Risk:** Carbonation bubbles are too prominent or too subtle.  
**Mitigation:** Ensure bubbles are captured in-camera or composited with proper light refraction. They should be visible and active-looking (freshness cue) but not distracting; aim for a few dozen small bubbles distributed evenly in the liquid.

**Risk:** The image reads as "expensive" rather than "premium contemporary."  
**Mitigation:** Avoid gold, jewel tones, ornate typography, or luxury cues (leather, velvet). Premium = clean, clear, confident simplicity. The mosambi *is* the luxury.

**Risk:** Bottle placement feels off-centre or awkward in 4:5.  
**Mitigation:** Test framing in-camera. Place the bottle cap at roughly 1/4 to 1/3 down from the top; leave 60%+ of the frame below it for negative space. The lower weight makes the format feel intentional and modern, not "small product in a big frame."

---

### HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK

✓ **4:5 vertical aspect ratio:** Confirmed.  
✓ **Product is the unmistakable hero:** Yes; framing, lighting, and composition center the bottle and liquid entirely.  
✓ **No clichéd "Indian" visual shorthand:** Yes; aesthetic is minimalist/contemporary, no decorative cultural elements, no heritage colour palette.  
✓ **Sophisticated and refined:** Yes; clean lighting, neutral palette, generous negative space, sharp bottle, premium material language.  
✓ **Refreshing:** Yes; carbonation, condensation, mosambi colour, cool palette.  
✓ **Culturally contemporary:** Yes; minimalism is globally current, mosambi is the cultural signifier (ingredient, not décor).  
✓ **Retains familiarity of mosambi:** Yes; visible fruit inside and/or as garnish; colour unmistakable to Indian consumers.  
✓ **Does not look cheap:** Yes; studio lighting, clean composition, premium materials and finish.  
✓ **No celebrity:** Confirmed, none included.  
✓ **No external website integration:** Confirmed, none required.

## FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

### DELIVERABLE
One premium 4:5 vertical still image for paid social and mobile display advertising. Product-forward, editorial in feel, no text overlay.

---

### OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
Convince urban Indian consumers aged 22–35 that this is a premium, contemporary beverage choice—not mass-market soft drink. Signal refreshment, modernity, and connection to the mosambi (sweet lime) ingredient without resorting to decorative "Indian" tropes. Make the product glass unmistakably the visual anchor; let it anchor trust and desirability.

---

### CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
**The Conversation Between Fruit and Refinement**

A single tall sparkling drink glass, half-filled with bright pale-amber liquid and visible carbonation, sits in neutral/cool light. One fresh mosambi fruit—cut or whole, unblemished, jewel-like—is positioned adjacent or slightly behind the glass, in sharp focus. Minimal negative space. No garnish clutter. The composition reveals:
- The drink's genuine colour and clarity (not artificial sweetness)
- The recognisable mosambi fruit as proof of ingredient authenticity
- Modern glassware suggesting premium positioning
- Restraint and craft, not volume or excess

The image reads as "sophisticated everyday" rather than "luxury spectacle" or "mass-market party."

---

### MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. **Primary:** The drink (glass, colour, carbonation) as the hero and trust signal
2. **Secondary:** The mosambi fruit as the cultural/ingredient anchor and proof of identity
3. **Tertiary:** Cleanliness, light, contemporary aesthetic (no text needed)

The mosambi does not illustrate the drink; it *validates* it. The drink's appearance sells the experience.

---

### VISUAL_SYSTEM

**Colour palette:**
- Pale warm gold to amber liquid (realistic mosambi sparkling water colour, not over-saturated)
- Cool whites, soft greys, or warm off-whites for background and negative space
- Mosambi: natural greens/yellows, matte and saturated
- No pattern, texture overload, or ornamental geometry

**Lighting:**
- Soft north-light or diffused directional light (no harsh shadows)
- Light enters the glass from above-left to show carbonation bubbles and liquid clarity
- Slight rim light on the glass edge to signal premium glassware
- Mosambi evenly lit, no hard shadows

**Composition:**
- Vertical 4:5 format: glass occupies the upper two-thirds (liquid filling roughly the middle zone), mosambi positioned lower-left or lower-right, slightly overlapping the glass base or sitting independently below
- Breathing room: 15–20% of frame is negative space (white or neutral)
- No clutter; no ice, no straw, no secondary objects

**Typography:**
- None in the image itself

---

### PRODUCTION_RECIPE

1. **Set dressing:** Clean white or pale grey seamless background (paper or painted wall). Minimal reflector fill; key light from above at 45°, soft diffusion.

2. **Glassware:** Tall, refined glass (straight or very slightly tapered). No branding visible. Fill with prepared mosambi sparkling water (or craft a visually identical mixture: pale golden sparkling water + mosambi juice or food colouring to exact the right tone). Ensure carbonation is visible; shoot soon after preparation.

3. **Fruit:** One fresh mosambi, unblemished. Shoot it whole and in-focus adjacent to the glass. Optional: one cross-section showing interior, placed nearby. Keep skin natural; do not over-oil or polish.

4. **Camera position:** Slightly above tabletop height (15–20° angle down); centred on the glass, mosambi in secondary focus zone.

5. **Post-production:** Minimal retouching. Correct exposure and colour balance to ensure the liquid reads as fresh and natural, not artificial or oversaturated. Sharpen the fruit. Leave natural skin texture on the mosambi. Slight vignette acceptable to draw eye to centre. Crop to exact 4:5.

---

### GENERATION_PROMPTS

**Final execution prompt:**

*A 4:5 vertical premium product photograph of a tall glass containing pale golden sparkling mosambi (sweet lime) water, carbonation bubbles clearly visible, positioned in the upper-centre portion of the frame. One fresh, whole mosambi fruit, matte-skinned, green-yellow, sits in sharp focus to the lower left of the glass base, slightly overlapped by the glass edge. Background is cool white or soft grey, seamless and empty. Soft diffused daylight from above illuminates the glass rim and liquid clarity. The fruit is naturally lit with no shiny oil. No text, no garnish, no ice, no secondary objects. Minimal negative space. Photography style: editorial, refined, contemporary. Colour grading: warm gold for the liquid, true greens and yellows for the fruit. Sharp, clean, modern. High-end beverage advertising aesthetic, not mass-market.*

---

### DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS

- **Mosambi fruit reference image(s):** Source one or two clear photographs of fresh mosambi from a library or supplier to ensure the model understands the fruit's exact appearance (colour, skin texture, size relative to the glass).
- **Glass reference:** Provide an image of the target glassware (tall, refined, minimalist—e.g., a simple tumbler or highball-style glass) to anchor the generation.
- **Colour reference:** Supply a swatch or sample image of the target liquid colour (pale golden amber) to guide hue.

These references act as visual briefs; they do not become part of the final image.

---

### AUDIO_AND_EDIT

Not applicable. This is a still image for social and mobile display.

---

### FAILURE_PREVENTION

**Failure modes and guardrails:**

1. **Overdesigned/cluttered image:** If the generation adds garnish, secondary fruit, condensation droplets, or a busy background, regenerate with explicit instruction: "Minimal. Nothing but the glass, the drink, and one mosambi fruit. Empty background. No clutter."

2. **Artificial or neon liquid colour:** If the liquid appears too bright, over-saturated, or fluorescent, request: "The sparkling water should be pale warm gold, natural-looking, not artificially bright. Like fresh pressed mosambi juice, not candy."

3. **Mosambi unrecognisable:** If the fruit looks generic or wrong, supply the reference image and request: "The fruit is a mosambi—pale yellow-green, round, thin skin, citrus. Match the provided reference image exactly."

4. **Text or branding creep:** If the model adds a product label, text, or brand mark, explicitly state: "No text. No labels. No branding visible. Image only."

5. **Wrong aspect ratio:** Ensure the final crop is exactly 4:5 (vertical, 1000 × 1250 pixels for digital use, or to client spec).

6. **Cheap or generic aesthetic:** If the image reads as a mass-market soft drink ad (gaudy, plastic, lifestyle-heavy), regenerate with: "Premium, editorial aesthetic. Refined simplicity. This is a contemporary craft beverage, not a mass-market product. Minimalist composition. Think luxury sparkling water or premium juice, not cola."

7. **Wrong lighting:** If shadows are harsh or the glass looks opaque, request: "Soft, diffused light. The glass and liquid must be crystal clear and luminous. Show the carbonation. Rim light on the glassware to emphasise premium quality."

---

### HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK

✅ **Format:** 4:5 vertical  
✅ **Product as hero:** Drink glass is the unmistakable focal point  
✅ **Mosambi included:** Fruit present and recognisable  
✅ **No clichéd "Indian" visual shorthand:** No flags, patterns, Taj Mahal, peacocks, or decorative geometry  
✅ **Sophisticated, not cheap:** Minimal, editorial, refined aesthetic; premium glassware; clean light  
✅ **Refreshing & contemporary:** Cool light, visible carbonation, modern composition  
✅ **Culturally familiar yet contemporary:** Mosambi is recognisable to the target audience and grounds the product in Indian citrus tradition; the execution is globally contemporary  
✅ **No celebrity:** None present  
✅ **No external website:** No links, QR codes, or URLs  
✅ **No text overlay:** Image is self-sufficient

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

---

**DELIVERABLE**
Single 4:5 vertical premium advertising image (still photography). Photorealistic execution. Delivery: High-resolution master file suitable for digital and print deployment.

---

**OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION**
Create a hero-product advertisement that positions a mosambi sparkling drink as a premium, contemporary beverage for affluent urban Indian adults. The image must:
- Make the product bottle/glass the unmistakable visual focal point
- Communicate refreshment, sophistication, and modernity simultaneously
- Signal "mosambi" without relying on temple bells, marigolds, or other visual clichés
- Avoid soft-drink-aisle positioning; position toward premium RTD beverages

---

**CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA**
A sun-lit, minimalist still-life moment capturing the product in its most essential, desirable state: a clear glass or bottle filled with translucent mosambi liquid and visible carbonation, set in an understated, light-filled environment that feels aspirational and contemporary. The mosambi's natural golden-amber color and the optical properties of carbonation (bubbles, refraction) carry the entire cultural and sensory narrative. No lifestyle prop clutter.

---

**MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY**
1. **Primary:** Product form, color, clarity, fizz—the drink itself as the singular hero
2. **Secondary:** Golden-amber tone of mosambi (signals origin fruit; no artificiality)
3. **Tertiary:** Lightness, clarity, premium glassware—signals sophistication and quality
4. **Avoided:** "Indian-ness" as visual decoration; narrative clutter; lifestyle excess

---

**VISUAL_SYSTEM**
- **Color palette:** Warm whites, soft cream/off-whites, and the natural warm-golden translucency of mosambi liquid. Minimal secondary colors.
- **Lighting:** High-key, directional natural light (or simulation thereof) that emphasizes the liquid's golden tone and carbonation bubble refraction. No dramatic shadows; clarity and luminosity are the mood.
- **Composition:** 4:5 vertical frame. Product positioned in the upper-to-middle third of frame, angled to maximize optical interest (bubble visibility, liquid translucency, rim/edge detail). Negative space below or around to emphasize premium positioning and breathing room.
- **Glassware/Vessel:** Either a modern, simple clear glass (highball, rocks, or bespoke taper) or a minimal, contemporary bottle with clean typography. No ornate glassware; no embellishment. The product's color and clarity must be the visual protagonist.
- **Surface/Environment:** Seamless or softly graduated light background (white, cream, or very pale warm tone). A single, subtle secondary surface (light marble, pale stone, or diffuse wood) is optional but only if it does not compete with the product. Prefer simplicity.
- **Depth/Focus:** Selective focus (shallow depth of field acceptable) on the glass/bottle to isolate it from environment. Ensure carbonation bubbles and liquid meniscus are tack-sharp.

---

**PRODUCTION_RECIPE**
1. **Setup:** Light tent or continuous studio lighting configured for high-key backlit or rim-lit clarity (emphasizes liquid translucency and bubbles). Position key light at 45° to front-left to catch refraction and fizz.
2. **Glassware:** Source a clean, contemporary clear glass or minimal bottle with modern typography (if bottled). Fill with actual mosambi juice + sparkling water or a visually equivalent stand-in (ensure golden-amber color is authentic).
3. **Bubbles:** Use fresh carbonation (immediately before shoot) or, if using still photography standin, fine food-grade glycerin + clear gel + micro air-bubble suspension for lasting bubble visibility in-frame.
4. **Styling:** No garnish (no mint, no citrus wheel, no straw). Product only. Optional: single droplet or condensation on exterior glass to signal cold/freshness, but only if subtle and not gestural.
5. **Camera:** High-resolution (minimum 4000px on long edge). Shoot tethered to monitor for real-time bubble and refraction verification.
6. **Post-production:** Minimal retouching. Enhance clarity of liquid, deepen mosambi golden tone slightly (if needed), ensure background is clean and white-balanced. No artificial enhancement of bubbles unless they have faded; maintain photorealism.

---

**GENERATION_PROMPTS**

**Final Execution Prompt:**

"4:5 vertical product photography. A crystal-clear modern glass filled with golden-amber mosambi sparkling drink, effervescent bubbles visible throughout the liquid, shot in bright natural or studio light from a 45-degree angle. The glass is positioned in the center-right upper half of the frame against a seamless soft white or cream background. No garnish, no other objects, no people. Shallow depth of field focuses on the glass and liquid; carbonation bubbles are sharp and luminous. The mosambi's warm, natural golden tone and the light refraction through the carbonated liquid are the sole visual interest. Photorealistic, premium beverage aesthetic. High resolution, professional product photography style. Contemporary and sophisticated. 4:5 aspect ratio."

---

**DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS**
- **Product name/branding:** If typography or label is required on glassware, this must be applied or composited post-generation (not trusted to generative model). Source the actual typography/logo artwork and composite it cleanly in post.
- **Liquid color calibration:** Mosambi's precise golden-amber tone should be verified against actual mosambi juice color reference before final approval. Generative color rendition may shift; post-process color-correct to reference.
- **Bubble density and distribution:** Generative models often struggle with realistic carbonation patterns. Expect manual post-editing or consider hybrid approach: generate base, photograph real carbonation separately, composite in.
- **Glassware shape:** If a specific bottle or glass design is mandated, provide reference photography to model to ensure form accuracy.

---

**AUDIO_AND_EDIT**
Not applicable (static image). No audio or editorial overlay required.

---

**FAILURE_PREVENTION**
1. **Product visibility:** If the background becomes too warm or visually busy, the mosambi liquid will read as generic amber liquid rather than distinctive mosambi. Enforce strict white/cream background discipline.
2. **Carbonation realism:** Generative models often produce "fake" bubble patterns (too uniform, too small, or spatially incoherent). Reference real carbonation photography and adjust post-generation.
3. **Luxury slide:** Avoid over-styling. A single garnish, a fancy coaster, or a second object tips the image into "premium soft drink" territory. Enforce product-only rule in review.
4. **Cultural authenticity vs. cliché:** Do not add cultural signifiers (texture, pattern, color, compositional trope) in an attempt to signal "Indian-ness." The mosambi itself is the cultural anchor; let it rest there.
5. **Liquid clarity:** If the liquid appears murky, opaque, or has a plastic look, regenerate with explicit instruction for glass-like transparency and natural juice tone.

---

**HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK**
- ✅ 4:5 aspect ratio
- ✅ Premium positioning (no cheap soft-drink aesthetic)
- ✅ Product as unmistakable hero
- ✅ Mosambi familiarity retained (golden-amber tone, carbonation signal)
- ✅ Culturally contemporary (minimalist, light-filled, no ethnic visual shorthand)
- ✅ No celebrity
- ✅ No external website reference
- ✅ Single concept (no alternatives provided)
- ✅ Executable and production-ready

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

---

**DELIVERABLE**

One premium 4:5 (vertical) e-commerce hero image, static, RGB 8-bit, suitable as primary product showcase on high-end product page. Aspect ratio critical for mobile and desktop banner use.

---

**OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION**

Produce a single photograph-realistic image that showcases the Aster Meridian 38 as a genuine, desirable mechanical watch. The image must prioritize dial legibility, material authenticity (brushed steel, leather, domed crystal), and wrist-wear context while maintaining clean commercial composition. The goal is earned premium positioning through honest product display, not artifice.

---

**CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA**

**Wrist-centric, dial-forward composition**: The watch is worn on a relaxed wrist, photographed from a 3/4 angle that places the dial at ~60° to camera. This stance is natural (not floating), demonstrates proportional wearability at 38mm, shows the leather strap authentically, and positions the dial's sunburst finish as the hero visual. Lighting is clean, directional, and reveals the domed sapphire crystal and case brushing without theatricality.

---

**MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY**

1. **Primary**: Dial design and sunburst finish (color, legibility, hand-marker contrast)
2. **Secondary**: Case material authenticity (brushed stainless steel surface quality)
3. **Tertiary**: Leather strap texture, case proportionality (38mm scale credible on wrist)
4. **Atmospheric**: Craftsmanship is implied through honest lighting and material rendering

No overlays, no text, no floating badges. The product speaks.

---

**VISUAL_SYSTEM**

- **Color palette**: Deep blue sunburst dial is hero; neutral gray-white background (no high contrast color distraction); brown leather strap warm anchor
- **Lighting direction**: Slightly raked (20–30° from dial plane) to emphasize sapphire dome reflection and brushed case texture; avoids flat, shadowless rendering
- **Focus and depth**: Watch dial and case in critical focus; wrist/strap softer but legible (shallow depth of field acceptable, ~50mm equivalent focal length framing)
- **Background**: Soft, neutral (light gray or off-white gradient); no props, no context, no lifestyle clutter
- **Texture and finish**: Brushed steel surface micro-detail visible; leather grain visible on strap; sapphire dome catches light subtly (no blown highlight)

---

**PRODUCTION_RECIPE**

1. **Watch positioning**: Placed on a real human wrist (or wrist form matching adult male proportions), approximately face-up at 45° tilt, rotated so dial faces 3/4 toward camera. Strap drapes naturally; no cinching or artificial tension.
2. **Lighting setup**:
   - Key light (soft, medium softbox or diffused): positioned 30° left-of-dial, 25° above horizontal, at ~2 meters. Intensity sufficient to render dial detail and catch sapphire dome.
   - Fill light (gentle, no hard shadow): opposite side, ~0.5 intensity ratio; prevents dark side of case from reading as black void.
   - No back light; watch should not feel separated from background.
3. **Camera position**: Perpendicular to wrist plane, framed to include watch and ~15–20 cm of wrist/lower forearm above and below. 4:5 framing places watch off-center slightly (dial in upper-middle third of frame).
4. **Optical**: 50mm equivalent focal length, f/4–f/5.6 aperture (dial and case sharp, subtle falloff on forearm).
5. **Post-production**: Neutral white balance (daylight temp); subtle contrast and clarity to emphasize dial sunburst and case brushing; no heavy saturation, no HDR look; color accuracy for blue dial and brown leather essential.

---

**GENERATION_PROMPTS**

**FINAL EXECUTION PROMPT** (for image generation):

"Premium product photography of a 38mm mechanical wristwatch, worn on a relaxed human wrist. Watch features a deep blue sunburst dial with silver baton hour markers and silver dauphine hands, no date window, domed sapphire crystal, brushed stainless-steel case, and dark brown leather strap. Camera angle is 3/4 view, dial-forward, wrist slightly tilted. Lighting is soft and directional, revealing dial detail, sapphire dome reflection, and brushed case texture. Background is neutral light gray, uncluttered. Photograph style, realistic materials, professional watch photography, 4:5 vertical aspect ratio."

---

**DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS**

- **Watch geometry**: Case diameter 38mm, dial color and marker positions, hand shapes, strap attachment—these are product facts and must not be altered by generation process. If generation fails to preserve specs, reject and regenerate with stricter constraints.
- **Strap color**: Dark brown, not tan or red-brown.
- **Dial detail**: Sunburst finish must be visible (radial brushing pattern); not flat or matte.
- **Wrist context**: Real human wrist, proportionally appropriate to 38mm case (credible fit, not oversized).

---

**AUDIO_AND_EDIT**

Not applicable. Static image.

---

**FAILURE_PREVENTION**

- **Dial illegibility**: If dial reads dark or sunburst pattern is invisible, reject. Generation should emphasize luminosity and radial texture.
- **Floating product**: If watch appears weightless or suspended, reject. Wrist must read as natural support.
- **Oversized case appearance**: If 38mm reads as 44mm or larger, reject. Reference proportional wrist-to-case ratio in prompt or retry.
- **Strap material confusion**: If leather strap reads as rubber, fabric, or synthetic, reject.
- **Sapphire dome absent**: If dome reads flat or crystal boundary is invisible, reject; dome's curvature and reflectivity are material proof-points.
- **Artificial lighting**: If image reads as 3D render or CGI, reject. Aim for photographic authenticity.

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

---

**DELIVERABLE**  
One 4:5 vertical e-commerce hero image (2880 × 3600 px minimum) of the Aster Meridian 38 mechanical watch, suitable for premium product-page use.

---

**OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION**  
Produce a single, commercially viable hero image that sells the Aster Meridian 38 by foregrounding its dial detail, material craft (brushed stainless, leather), and wearable desirability without misrepresenting product geometry. The 4:5 ratio is optimal for mobile e-commerce and premium placement. Avoid decorative CGI excess; emphasize what is genuinely there.

---

**CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA**  
**Wrist-mounted close detail shot with angled top-down perspective.**  
The watch is positioned on a natural human wrist at approximately 45° angle, tilted slightly to catch light on the domed sapphire and the sunburst dial structure. Shallow depth of field focuses on dial and case; wrist and leather strap ground the product in desirability context. No floating watch, no abstract backgrounds—just the watch as it exists, lit to reveal its actual material qualities. This approach respects the product geometry, communicates wear-ability immediately, and avoids the clinical coldness of isolated floating CGI.

---

**MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY**  
1. **Primary:** Sunburst dial detail (blue depth, light reflection, baton marker geometry)  
2. **Secondary:** Case finish (brushed stainless texture, dome of sapphire crystal)  
3. **Tertiary:** Leather strap and wrist context (anchors desirability, shows scale, implies craftsmanship)  
4. **Implicit:** Precision (no date clutter, refined simplicity, dauphine hands)

---

**VISUAL_SYSTEM**  
- **Color palette:** Deep blue sunburst as hero color; warm neutral skin tone; brown leather; silvery-gray brushed stainless catching highlights  
- **Lighting:** Single-key light source (approximately 10–11 o'clock) angled to catch the domed sapphire edge, bounce light into the sunburst dial, and reveal the brushed finish of the case without washing it out. Cool (5000–5500K) to emphasize the blue and steel. Slight backlight on leather strap to separate it.  
- **Surface/Context:** Very subtle, neutral background (soft gradient or soft-focus blurred environment—e.g., a neutral interior). The focus is entirely on the watch and wrist. Avoid reflective surfaces that distract.  
- **Texture emphasis:** Sapphire crystal should show a clear reflection line or subtle refraction; brushed stainless should be matte but with directional grain visible; leather should show grain and natural wear patina (not laboratory pristine).

---

**PRODUCTION_RECIPE**

**Option A (Photographic, preferred if product access available):**  
1. Source a Seiko 5 or similar 38 mm benchmark watch for geometry reference (if the Aster Meridian 38 does not physically exist yet).  
2. Style watch on a model's or stand-in wrist with a natural, comfortable pose.  
3. Prop: dark brown leather strap positioned to show fold and natural form.  
4. Lighting: Single key light at 45° from upper left; fill bounce from opposite side at low intensity; possible backlight for strap separation.  
5. Shoot tethered to a monitor; capture 20–30 frames with slight angle and focus adjustments to isolate dial detail and case finish.  
6. Post-production: Color-correct to emphasize the deep blue sunburst (slight saturation and contrast boost on blue channel); enhance brushed-stainless texture subtly (micro-contrast); warm the wrist/skin tone slightly; vignette very softly to center focus on watch; ensure sapphire shows no dust or fingerprints.

**Option B (High-fidelity 3D render if watch is not yet manufactured):**  
1. Build a precise 3D model of the Aster Meridian 38 from the given specs: 38 mm case diameter, exact proportions of baton markers and dauphine hands, domed sapphire, brushed metal topology on case, brown leather strap geometry.  
2. Use a high-end renderer (e.g., Octane, V-Ray, or equivalent) with physically accurate materials: brushed stainless (anisotropic reflection), sunburst dial (radial texture with micro-geometry), sapphire (high IOR, Fresnel), leather (subsurface scattering, grain map).  
3. Position the watch on a photogrammetry-sourced wrist model or a procedural hand asset.  
4. Apply the same lighting setup as Option A (single key + fill + subtle backlight).  
5. Render at 2880 × 3600 with 16-bit color depth and minimal noise; use denoisers conservatively to preserve texture.  
6. Composite: place the rendered watch+wrist on a subtle background (gradient or soft interior blur); add any necessary shadow-catch geometry to ground the wrist naturally.

**Either path:**  
- Avoid any clone-stamp or object-removal editing that alters case geometry, strap shape, or hand/marker positions.  
- Preserve product accuracy above aesthetic softness.  
- Final color grade should feel premium but not surreal (no heavy saturation, unrealistic contrast, or plastic sheen).

---

**GENERATION_PROMPTS**

**For photographic/on-wrist shoot direction:**  
> A 38 mm brushed stainless steel mechanical watch with a deep blue sunburst dial, silver baton hour markers, silver dauphine hands, and a domed sapphire crystal. Dark brown leather strap. Photographed on a natural wrist, angled 45° toward camera, with studio key light from 10 o'clock. Focus tightly on the dial detail and case finish. Warm, neutral background (interior light blur). Shallow depth of field. Emphasize the texture of the brushed metal and the depth of the blue sunburst dial. No date window visible. Commercial product photography, premium watch brand aesthetic.

**For 3D render:**  
> A high-fidelity 3D render of a 38 mm mechanical watch. Case: brushed stainless steel with anisotropic surface reflection. Dial: deep blue sunburst with radial gradient texture and micro-geometry. Hour markers: slim silver baton indices. Hands: silver dauphine (sword-shape). Sapphire crystal: clear, domed, with realistic Fresnel reflection. Strap: dark brown leather with grain texture and natural patina. Positioned on a natural human wrist, 45° angle tilted slightly toward light source. Studio key light from upper left at 45°. No floating product, no abstract background, no date window. Commercial product render, premium horological craftsmanship emphasis.

---

**DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS**  
- **Case diameter:** Exactly 38 mm (scaling must be accurate).  
- **Dial color:** Deep blue sunburst (no purple, no light blue, no flat blue).  
- **Hand and marker geometry:** Silver baton markers (not applied indices, not lume-filled circles), silver dauphine hands (pointed, not skeleton, not Mercedes-style).  
- **No date window** (no cutout on dial at 3 o'clock or anywhere else).  
- **Sapphire crystal:** Must visually read as domed/curved, not flat.  
- **Strap:** Dark brown leather (not tan, not black, not fabric). Must show strap as it naturally drapes or folds on wrist.  
- **Case finish:** Brushed stainless, not polished; directionality of brush pattern should be visible under magnification.  

*These are non-negotiable product identity constraints.* Any generative tool output that omits, simplifies, or casually re-interprets these details must be rejected and re-prompted or re-shot.

---

**AUDIO_AND_EDIT**  
Not applicable. This is a static image.

---

**FAILURE_PREVENTION**  
1. **Dial color drift:** Use a color reference card in setup; confirm blue is saturated and cool-toned (not muddy, not purple). If using generative tools, include explicit color-hex reference (e.g., #0B3D7D or similar deep blue) in prompts.  
2. **Hand/marker legibility:** Ensure baton markers and dauphine hands are not blurred or obscured by depth-of-field. Sharpen selectively during post if needed, but do not artificially enhance beyond physical realism.  
3. **Sapphire clarity:** If photographing, clean crystal obsessively before shoot. If rendering, add a realistic reflection line or subtle lens flare to signal the dome without overstatement.  
4. **Strap geometry:** If photographic, ensure leather is positioned naturally (not artificially stretched or kinked). If rendered, use a real leather shader with displacement and normal maps, not a flat material.  
5. **Wrist integration:** Avoid uncanny hand geometry (wrong proportions, unnatural angle). If rendering, use a credible hand asset; if photographing, use a genuine model and frame so proportions read naturally.  
6. **Aspect ratio:** Lock 4:5 early. Do not crop or re-frame at the end, as this risks losing critical product detail or introducing compositional imbalance.  
7. **Generative artifact rejection:** If using AI-generative imagery, do a final pixel-by-pixel comparison of dial markings, strap stitching, and case geometry against the specs. Reject any where the watch has been "perfected" or subtly re-designed (e.g., fewer markers, different hand shapes, missing texture).

---

**HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK**  
✓ Aspect ratio: 4:5 (locked)  
✓ Product facts: All six specs (38 mm, brushed stainless, deep blue sunburst, silver baton markers, silver dauphine hands, no date, domed sapphire, dark brown leather) preserved exactly  
✓ Commercial utility: Wrist-mounted, well-lit detail shot is standard for premium watch e-commerce; works on product page, mobile, and promotional use  
✓ Avoids generic floating-product CGI: Wrist-mounted and grounded in real or realistic context  
✓ No external websites: All direction and references are self-contained  
✓ Craftsmanship, dial detail, material quality, desirability: All emphasized via lighting, angle, context, and texture reveal

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

---

**DELIVERABLE**

One 4:5 static image: premium e-commerce hero photograph/render of the Aster Meridian 38 mechanical watch, ready for product page hero placement.

---

**OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION**

Create a single, commercially viable hero image that displays the watch as a desirable, well-crafted object while communicating material quality and dial detail through professional context and lighting. The image must be faithful to the product's geometry and finish specifications and must work as the primary image on a luxury product page.

---

**CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA**

On-wrist studio shot at controlled angle, showing the watch in its functional context (worn) rather than as a floating object. This approach:
- Demonstrates actual desirability and wearability
- Avoids generic CGI floating-product cliché
- Provides natural human scale reference
- Allows precise control of lighting to reveal sunburst dial, sapphire dome, and brushed-steel finish
- Fills 4:5 format efficiently with wrist, watch face, and forearm in soft focus

---

**MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY**

1. **Primary**: Watch dial face, sunburst pattern, baton markers, dauphine hands—the core visual identity
2. **Secondary**: Brushed stainless-steel case finish, domed sapphire crystal clarity and reflection
3. **Tertiary**: Dark brown leather strap, wrist context (implies craftsmanship, precision, wearability)
4. **Implied**: 38 mm case size (human wrist provides scale reference)

---

**VISUAL_SYSTEM**

- **Framing**: Wrist angled 35–45° toward camera, watch face dominant in frame, forearm/wrist occupying lower third to half of image. Compose for 4:5 crop.
- **Background**: Soft, neutral, slightly textured (matte white, warm grey, or soft natural fabric). No competing detail. Depth sufficient to separate forearm from background (shallow depth of field acceptable).
- **Lighting**: Three-point studio setup:
  - **Key light** (angled 45° above and to one side): Reveals sunburst dial pattern, catches sapphire dome reflection without glare, models brushed-steel case texture
  - **Fill light** (soft, opposite key): Gently fills shadows; avoids harsh contrast on dial
  - **Top/back accent** (optional, subtle): Defines dome edge and strap separation
- **Color palette**: Deep blue sunburst, brushed silver-grey, warm dark brown leather, neutral background. Warm color temperature (~3200–4500 K) to complement leather and dial warmth.
- **Surface detail**: Ensure sunburst brushing on dial is visible; sapphire dome must show subtle reflection and clarity; brushed case surfaces show linear texture; leather strap shows natural grain.

---

**PRODUCTION_RECIPE**

1. **Source material**: Photorealistic 3D model of Aster Meridian 38 (geometry locked; all specifications as stated) + human wrist/forearm model OR photograph a calibrated physical watch mock-up on a model's wrist if available.
2. **Setup**: Mount watch on wrist at 35–45° angle (not flat, not extreme). Position so:
   - Watch face is clearly visible and fills ~40–50% of frame width
   - Wrist/forearm occupies lower portion
   - Sapphire dome faces light source at slight angle for optimal reflection
3. **Lighting**: Soft studio lights (octabox or similar for key; diffusion for fill). Avoid harsh shadows on dial. Test lighting angle to confirm sunburst pattern is visible and dome has subtle catch-light.
4. **Post-production**: Color grading for warm, luxury aesthetic. Mild contrast enhancement. Sharpness on dial face and case; slight softness on background. No artificial blur or unrealistic processing. Ensure product geometry remains unaltered.
5. **Cropping**: 4:5 aspect ratio, centered on watch face with balanced negative space.

---

**GENERATION_PROMPTS**

**Final Image Prompt** (single executable):

"Premium studio photograph of the Aster Meridian 38 mechanical watch worn on a human wrist, shot at 4:5 aspect ratio. The watch is positioned at a 40-degree angle to the camera, watch face dominant. Key details: 38 mm brushed stainless-steel case, deep blue sunburst dial pattern visible and well-lit, silver baton hour markers, silver dauphine hands showing craftsmanship, no date window, domed sapphire crystal with subtle reflection and clarity, dark brown leather strap. Wrist and forearm occupy lower portion of frame with soft neutral background (warm grey or off-white) in soft focus. Professional three-point studio lighting: key light angled 45° above to reveal sunburst texture and dome reflection, fill light soft and opposite, no harsh shadows. Warm color temperature (~4000 K). Shallow depth of field separates wrist from background. Photograph style: luxury watchmaking, commercial product imagery, sharp on dial and case, soft on background. No floating product, no CGI artifacts, no color or geometry alterations. Brand-new condition, minimal reflections, professional finish."

---

**DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS**

- **Product geometry and specifications** (38 mm case, sunburst dial, baton markers, dauphine hands, sapphire dome, leather strap): non-negotiable and fully defined. These must be generated/rendered with fidelity.
- **Aspect ratio**: 4:5 (deterministic crop)
- **Composition rule**: Watch face dominant, wrist context, neutral background
- **Material surfaces**: Brushed steel, deep blue sunburst, dark brown leather (defined finishes, not to be altered)

---

**AUDIO_AND_EDIT**

Not applicable (static image).

---

**FAILURE_PREVENTION**

1. **Geometry drift**: Confirm case diameter, hand shape, marker style, and strap attachment against product spec before final render/shoot. No scaled or proportional shortcuts.
2. **Dial detail loss**: Ensure sunburst pattern is visible at final resolution (test at 2000+ px width minimum for e-commerce use).
3. **Sapphire dome**: Confirm it appears as a clear, domed surface with subtle reflection—not flat, not over-glossy, not invisible.
4. **Lighting failure**: Over-bright key light will wash out sunburst; too dim will hide texture. Test render/shoot in multiple angles before committing.
5. **Background distraction**: Ensure background remains neutral and soft; any pattern or color should complement, not compete.
6. **Wrist realism**: If using 3D wrist model, ensure skin tone, proportions, and angle feel natural. If photographing, ensure model's hand/wrist is relaxed and properly positioned.
7. **Color cast**: Avoid cool/blue casts that flatten the dial; warm tone should feel luxurious, not filtered or artificial.
8. **File spec**: Deliver at minimum 2400 × 3000 px (4:5, 300 DPI equivalent or 72 DPI @10" × 8" minimum) for e-commerce hero use.

---

# CONTEST IMG07 — B02

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) static poster, 4:5 aspect ratio (recommended output 1080×1350 px, scalable to 2160×2700 for print), single final composited file (PNG/PDF-ready). No alternate versions.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The client (Aight) needs a festive-season acquisition poster whose entire job is to make two numbers — ₹9 for image generation and ₹99 for video generation — register instantly and credibly, while the overall artifact still reads as enterprise AI infrastructure (per aight.ai's actual brand voice: "Where Indian businesses buy AI," prepaid rupee wallet, hard caps, one domestic GST invoice, founder-level support) rather than an e-commerce sale banner. Success = a viewer understands both prices in under 2 seconds AND perceives the brand as a serious, trustworthy AI platform for businesses, not a consumer discount app.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Price Sheet, Not the Sale Banner." Treat the poster like a minimal enterprise price card — the kind a serious infra company (cloud billing, payments API) would publish — and let a single festive gesture (a warm gold light gradient, evoking diya/sunrise light without any literal festive iconography) be the only seasonal signal. Typography carries 90% of the message; the festive layer is atmospheric, not decorative.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Brand identity — small, quiet, top: "aight." wordmark (lowercase, period, exactly as brand uses it).
2. Seasonal context tag — small pill/label: "Festive Season Pricing" — establishes relevance without shouting "SALE."
3. THE OFFER (dominant visual mass, center):
   - "Image generation" → "₹9" (large)
   - "Video generation" → "₹99" (larger, since video is the higher-value/harder product — establishes clear price-value logic, not arbitrary numbers)
   Both prices set at near-equal massive scale so neither reads as an afterthought; differentiated by weight/position, not color gimmicks.
4. Brand proof line — mid-small: "Where Indian businesses buy AI." (verbatim brand tagline).
5. Trust footer — small caps: "Prepaid rupees · Hard caps · One domestic GST invoice."
6. CTA — bottom: "Talk to us" + "getaight.ai"

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: near-black charcoal (#0E0E10) base, warm marigold-gold (#E8A33D–#F2B84C gradient) as the single accent, off-white/ivory (#F4F1E8) for primary type. No red/green/multicolor festive clichés, no confetti, no rangoli/diya clip art.
- Light motif: a soft, large-radius warm gold glow rising from the lower third of the frame, blending into black — abstract enough to read as "premium ambient light" to a general audience and as "diya/festive warmth" to an Indian audience. This is the only "festive" cue.
- Typography: a single modern grotesk/sans family (e.g., a geometric-humanist sans in the style of Inter/Neue Haas). Numerals in bold/black weight, labels in regular/medium with wide tracking and small size — classic infra/SaaS pricing-page hierarchy (label small-caps above, numeral huge below).
- Grid: strict left-aligned or centered vertical grid, generous negative space top and sides, no clutter, no stock photography, no human figures — this is what keeps it "infrastructure" rather than "retail."
- Texture: very subtle fine grain/noise over the black field to avoid a flat digital-ad look and add print-poster quality.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Two-layer build for reliability and premium finish:
1. AI-generated BACKGROUND PLATE only — dark gradient field with the warm gold glow, subtle grain, no text at all (text-in-generation is unreliable at this precision).
2. Deterministic TYPOGRAPHY OVERLAY — all copy (wordmark, prices, labels, tagline, footer, CTA) set as vector/real text in a design tool and composited on top, using the exact brand copy captured from getaight.ai. This guarantees pixel-perfect, correctly-spelled, on-brand type — critical since typography is explicitly the hero device.
3. Final export at 4:5, color-graded to lock contrast between ivory text and charcoal field for legibility at thumbnail size (this poster will likely be viewed small on WhatsApp/social first).

GENERATION_PROMPTS
(Image deliverable — one construction plan, generation used only for the background plate; text applied deterministically as above.)

Final background-plate generation prompt:
"A premium, minimalist dark background for a high-end technology/infrastructure brand poster, 4:5 vertical aspect ratio. Deep charcoal-black base (#0E0E10) occupying the upper two-thirds, transitioning in the lower third into a soft, large, diffused warm marigold-gold glow (#E8A33D to #F2B84C) that fades upward into the black, evoking the warm ambient light of a diya or festive lamp without depicting any literal object, icon, flame, or pattern. No text, no logos, no people, no clutter, no confetti, no rangoli motifs, no product photography. Subtle fine film-grain texture across the whole frame for a premium print-poster finish. Smooth, controlled gradient, corporate and restrained rather than decorative — the mood of a serious fintech or cloud-infrastructure brand campaign during festive season, not a retail sale banner. Clean negative space in the upper two-thirds and center for future typography placement. Ultra-clean, high-resolution, subtle, sophisticated, editorial lighting quality."

Construction plan for full poster (post-generation, deterministic):
- Canvas 1080×1350 (4:5).
- Top margin ~8%: "aight." wordmark, left-aligned, small, ivory, medium weight.
- Top-right: small pill outline, "Festive Season Pricing," ivory text, thin gold border.
- Vertical center block (roughly 30%–75% of height):
  - "IMAGE GENERATION" (small-caps, wide tracking, ivory, ~24px equivalent) directly above "₹9" (bold, ~140px equivalent, ivory/near-white, positioned over the start of the gold glow for warmth).
  - Thin 1px gold horizontal rule beneath.
  - "VIDEO GENERATION" (small-caps) above "₹99" (bold, slightly larger than ₹9 to reflect it as the higher-tier product, ~160px equivalent), sitting fully within the gold-glow zone for maximum warmth/emphasis.
  - Small suffix "/ generation" in light weight, small size, right of or beneath each price.
- Lower band (~80%–100% of height): centered "Where Indian businesses buy AI." (medium size, ivory), then footer small-caps line "Prepaid rupees · Hard caps · One domestic GST invoice" (thin, muted ivory ~70% opacity), then bottom-most CTA row "Talk to us  —  getaight.ai" (small, gold-colored text for CTA emphasis).

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- All copy is fixed, sourced verbatim from getaight.ai where applicable, and must be set as real vector text in post-production, not generated as pixels: "aight.", "Where Indian businesses buy AI.", "Prepaid rupees · Hard caps · One domestic GST invoice", "Talk to us", "getaight.ai".
- Pricing copy "₹9" and "₹99" with labels "Image generation" / "Video generation" and "/ generation" suffix — fixed, exact, no rounding or restyling.
- Rupee symbol must render correctly (₹), verified manually — not left to the generation model.
- Color hex values and grid/margins above are fixed specs, not creative suggestions, to protect legibility.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static poster deliverable.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: AI-generated text renders garbled/misspelled prices → Mitigated by generating a text-free background plate and applying all copy deterministically.
- Risk: Festive treatment drifts into discount-flyer territory (starbursts, "SALE," multicolor confetti) → Mitigated by restricting palette to charcoal + single gold accent and banning festive clip-art/iconography in the prompt.
- Risk: Two prices compete/confuse hierarchy → Mitigated by giving both prices dominant, near-equal visual weight but placing "Video ₹99" slightly larger and inside the warmest part of the glow, since it's the higher-value product; both remain unmistakably legible at thumbnail size.
- Risk: Poster feels generic/tech-stocky → Mitigated by using brand's own verbatim language (from getaight.ai) for tagline and trust line, anchoring it specifically to Aight rather than a generic AI brand.
- Risk: Glow gradient overpowers text contrast → Mitigated by constraining glow to lower third and using ivory/near-white type with sufficient contrast checked at export.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Single deliverable, 4:5 poster: ✅
- ₹9 image / ₹99 video, both immediately understandable: ✅ (largest visual elements on the page)
- Premium AI-infrastructure feel, not discount-retail: ✅ (charcoal/gold minimal palette, infra-style pricing-card layout, no sale/discount visual clichés)
- Typography and hierarchy do most of the work: ✅ (single non-text visual motif; all meaning carried by type scale/weight/position)
- Only getaight.ai used for brand/product info: ✅ (wordmark, tagline, and trust claims sourced from the site content)
- No model/provider recommended: ✅
- Single concept only, no alternatives offered: ✅

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) static poster, 4:5 aspect ratio, print/social-ready master at 2160×2700px (scalable), RGB, for digital + optional print use.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer needs a single festive-season poster for Aight (getaight.ai) that makes ₹9 (image) and ₹99 (video) instantly legible as the headline commercial fact, while the overall design reads as enterprise AI infrastructure — closer to a cloud-platform pricing sheet than a sale banner. Per the site's own positioning ("Where Indian businesses buy AI," prepaid rupee wallet, hard caps, one domestic GST invoice, founder-led support), the brand voice is plain, confident, financially precise, and unglamorous-by-design. Festivity should be present as light/warmth, not as decoration that competes with the numbers.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Rate Card." Present the two prices exactly the way Aight already talks about billing on its site — as a clean, tabular, invoice-like line-item card (label left, tabular-numeral price right, thin rule between rows) — the same visual grammar as a cloud pricing page or a GST invoice. Festive season is expressed only as a single warm diya-glow gradient bleeding from behind the card, echoing "the counter is open" language from the brand without any retail-sale visual clichés (no confetti, starbursts, "OFFER," crossed-out prices, or generic Diwali clip art).

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Brand mark: "Aight ." (small, top-left, quiet)
2. Context line: "Festive season pricing" (small caps, top-right, understated — signals occasion without shouting sale)
3. HERO — the rate card (dominant visual weight, center):
   - "Image generation" — "₹9 / image"
   - thin rule
   - "Video generation" — "₹99 / video"
4. Credibility line (support, mid-lower third): "Prepaid rupees. One GST invoice. No surprise bills." — directly sourced from site language, reinforces "serious infra" not "discount."
5. CTA + URL (bottom, small, permanent-looking): "Talk to us — getaight.ai"
6. Legal/footer microtype (very small, bottom edge): "Ashva Intelligence Pvt Ltd · Prices per generation, prepaid wallet."

Typography carries the hierarchy through scale and weight jumps only — no icons, no illustration, no discount badges.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: near-black charcoal base (#0B0B0C), warm off-white text (#F4EFE8), single festive accent — deep amber/gold (#D8A24A to #F2C879 gradient) used only for the price digits and the background glow; a hairline stroke color (#3A3733) for rules/grid.
- Grid: strict 12-column layout, generous margins (≥8% of frame on all sides), everything left/right aligned to the grid — no floating or tilted elements.
- Type system: two families only.
  - Display/label sans: geometric grotesk, e.g. an Inter/Helvetica-Now-class face, medium/regular weight, wide tracking on small caps.
  - Numerals: monospace/tabular-figure face (invoice/ledger feel) for ₹9 and ₹99 specifically — largest type on the page, weight bold, color gradient gold-on-dark.
- Motif: one soft, radial diya-light gradient bloom positioned behind the rate card, warm amber fading to black — the only "festive" cue, no literal diya, rangoli, marigold, or lantern illustrations.
- Whitespace: minimum 40% of frame kept clean/dark to preserve premium, infra-brand restraint.
- No stock-photo people, no product screenshots, no AI/tech clichés (no glowing circuit boards, robots, or neon).

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Generate a textless background plate: dark charcoal gradient field with a single warm amber radial glow positioned at center-frame, subtle fine film-grain/noise texture, faint 1px grid hairlines suggested in the negative space (ledger/graph-paper feel), 4:5 composition, large clean negative-space zones reserved top, center, and bottom for text overlay. No typography, no logos, no numerals generated by the model (text accuracy is handled deterministically in post — see next section).
2. Composite deterministic typography, rate-card rules, brand wordmark, and CTA in a design tool (vector-based) using the exact copy specified above, on the grid specified above.
3. Color-correct so the amber glow reads festive without shifting the charcoal base warm/orange (keep base neutral-cool to preserve "infra," not "diwali sale").
4. Export master at 2160×2700 (4:5), plus locked-ratio scale-downs for social use.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single image — one base-plate generation prompt (background/lighting only; all text added deterministically afterward):

"Premium minimalist background for a serious enterprise AI-infrastructure poster, 4:5 vertical composition. Deep charcoal-black base (#0B0B0C) with a single soft radial glow of warm amber-gold light (#F2C879 to #0B0B0C) emanating from the center of the frame, evoking a single diya's warmth without any literal diya, flame, lantern, marigold or rangoli imagery. Extremely subtle fine film-grain texture across the whole frame. Faint, almost invisible thin horizontal hairlines suggested in the dark negative space, like a ledger or graph paper, reinforcing a financial/invoice mood. Generous clean empty space in the top third, exact center, and bottom third of the frame for typography to be added later — no text, no numbers, no logos, no icons, no people, no objects. Mood: quiet, confident, high-end B2B fintech/cloud-infrastructure aesthetic, not festive-retail, not decorative. Lighting: soft, controlled, single warm source, otherwise cool neutral dark. Ultra-clean, high resolution, no clutter, no vignette artifacts, no lens flares, no bokeh sparkles, no confetti, no glitter."

CONSTRUCTION PLAN for final compositing (deterministic layer, in order):
- Layer 1: generated background plate (above).
- Layer 2: top bar — "Aight ." left, "Festive season pricing" right, both small caps, off-white, 1px rule beneath.
- Layer 3: centered rate-card block — two rows with left-aligned labels ("Image generation," "Video generation") and right-aligned tabular numerals ("₹9 / image," "₹99 / video") in gold gradient, separated by one hairline rule; numerals rendered at roughly 4–5× the label type size to make price the unmistakable focal point.
- Layer 4: credibility line centered beneath the card, smaller off-white type: "Prepaid rupees. One GST invoice. No surprise bills."
- Layer 5: bottom CTA bar — "Talk to us — getaight.ai," small, permanent/utilitarian styling (not a "button," just confident type), plus micro-footer legal line at the very bottom edge.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- All typography, including both prices (₹9, ₹99), brand wordmark "Aight .", CTA, URL, and legal line, is set as vector text in a design tool — never left to the image generator — to guarantee 100% accuracy of numerals, currency symbol, spelling, and brand mark.
- Grid, rule lines, and card layout are built as deterministic vector shapes.
- Only the atmospheric background (glow, grain, hairline texture) is produced generatively; it contains no text or brand elements.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable, no audio/motion/edit required.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: AI-generated text misrendering price digits or currency symbol → mitigated by keeping all text fully deterministic/vector, generated art is text-free.
- Risk: design drifting into "discount flyer" territory (starbursts, "SALE," crossed-out prices) → explicitly excluded from copy and visual system; only one festive cue permitted (amber glow).
- Risk: festive glow overpowering premium/infra tone → constrained to a single soft radial bloom on neutral charcoal, not a warm-tinted overall palette.
- Risk: hierarchy confusion between two prices → enforced via identical row structure, aligned rule, and matched type scale so both prices read with equal, immediate clarity.
- Risk: brand tone mismatch → credibility line and CTA phrasing pulled directly from Aight's own site language (prepaid rupees, GST invoice, "Talk to us").

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Format: exactly one poster, 4:5 — met.
- Both prices (₹9 image, ₹99 video) immediately understandable — met via largest type on page, tabular alignment, dedicated rate-card structure.
- Feels like serious AI infra company, not discount retail flyer — met via ledger/invoice visual grammar, restrained palette, single festive cue, no retail-sale iconography.
- Typography and hierarchy do most of the work — met; no illustration/photography, only type, rules, and one atmospheric glow.
- Used only getaight.ai for brand/product facts — met.
- No model/provider named or recommended — met.
- Single concept only, no alternatives presented — met.

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) premium festive-season promotional poster, static image, 4:5 aspect ratio (deliver at 4000×5000 px for print/digital re-use), single final composited file (no alternates).

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The poster must make two exact numbers — ₹9 (image) and ₹99 (video) — instantly legible and unambiguous, because this is the entire commercial hook. At the same time it must read as an AI infrastructure product from a serious B2B platform (per getaight.ai: prepaid rupee wallet, hard caps, one domestic GST invoice, GPT/Claude/Gemini + India-first voice, founder-level support) — not a festive-sale flyer with starbursts and confetti. Festivity should be atmospheric and premium (light, warmth, prosperity), never discount-signage. Typography and hierarchy carry the message; imagery is restrained and supportive.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Festive Ledger." Aight's own brand language is built around a counter/invoice metaphor — "prepaid rupees," "one domestic GST invoice," "the merchant's job." The poster visualises the two prices as two clean ledger line-items on a dark, gold-foil invoice card, gently lit by a single diya — as if this is literally the line on the bill Indian businesses will actually pay this festive season. This ties the seasonal cue directly to the brand's real proof point (transparent, single, domestic billing) rather than to generic Diwali decoration.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Hero (largest, top-weighted visually within the ledger card): the two price lines —
   "IMAGE  ₹9" / "VIDEO  ₹99"
2. Brand lockup: "aight ." — small, top of frame, quietly confident
3. Positioning line (site-sourced): "Where Indian businesses buy AI."
4. Seasonal cue (subordinate, one line): "This festive season."
5. Proof/credibility strip (small, below ledger): "GPT · Claude · Gemini · India-first voice — prepaid rupees, one GST invoice."
6. CTA (bottom, clear but modest): "Talk to us · getaight.ai"

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Format: vertical 4:5, generous margins, strict grid — nothing touches edges.
- Palette: near-black charcoal ground (#121110); brass/gold foil (#C9A227) for rules, logo, numerals accents; warm amber diya glow (#FF8A3D at low opacity) as the only "festive colour"; ivory/parchment (#EDE6D8) for body copy. No red/orange discount-tag colours, no confetti, no fireworks, no percentage-off badges, no starbursts.
- Typography: two-family system.
  - Numerals/prices: a refined serif or slab with tabular oldstyle figures, set very large, letter-spaced tightly, treated like ledger/invoice figures (echoes "invoice line," not "sale tag").
  - Labels, tagline, CTA: a clean geometric/grotesk sans, small size, wide tracking, matching the minimal tech-site tone.
- Composition:
  - Top 15%: logotype "aight ." + tagline, small, centered or left-aligned.
  - Middle 55%: the ledger card — a thin gold-rule rectangle on charcoal, two rows separated by a hairline gold rule: row 1 "IMAGE — ₹9", row 2 "VIDEO — ₹99", each with a tiny qualifier beneath ("per generation"). One small diya glow motif sits quietly at one corner of the card, its light falling across the gold rule.
  - Lower 20%: proof strip + seasonal line, both small, then CTA at the very bottom, set apart by whitespace.
  - Bottom 10%: clear breathing margin, no clutter.
- Mood reference: annual-report cover meets premium fintech meets a single lit diya on a dark wood counter at night — restrained luxury, not a bazaar poster.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Two-layer build for accuracy and premium finish:
1. AI-generated atmospheric base layer: dark textured background (fine linen/paper-grain charcoal), a single soft diya glow, faint brass hairline rule graphics, deep vignette — generated with an explicit reserved negative-space grid so no AI-rendered text appears anywhere in this layer.
2. Deterministic typography overlay (see next section): logo, tagline, seasonal line, both price lines, qualifiers, proof strip and CTA are set as real vector/type layers and composited on top, guaranteeing ₹9 and ₹99 render pixel-perfect, correctly spelled, and consistently kerned — critical since generative models frequently corrupt currency symbols and numerals.
Final export: flatten to single 4:5 image, sRGB, print-safe contrast checked (gold-on-charcoal minimum contrast verified for both digital and print reproduction).

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single executable prompt for the AI-generated base/background art layer (text to be added deterministically afterward per Construction Plan above):

"Premium dark charcoal textured background, fine linen/paper grain, near-black tone (#121110). A single warm amber diya (small oil lamp) glowing softly in the lower-left third, its light casting a subtle warm gradient across the frame. Thin brass/gold hairline rules forming a minimal open rectangular ledger-card frame in the vertical center of the composition, with one horizontal brass hairline dividing the card into two equal rows. No text, no numerals, no logos, no people, no confetti, no fireworks, no discount badges, no bright saturated colors — only charcoal, brass/gold, and warm amber glow. Vertical 4:5 composition, generous empty negative space reserved at top 15%, inside both ledger rows, and bottom 20% for later typography. Mood: quiet, premium, infrastructural, festive through light rather than ornament, like a high-end fintech annual report cover lit by a single diya. Sharp focus, subtle film grain, soft cinematic lighting, no clutter, no gradients that clash with gold, high production value, minimalist luxury aesthetic."

Construction Plan (deterministic overlay, applied after base render):
- Top zone: "aight ." logotype (exact brand mark with trailing period) + "Where Indian businesses buy AI." in small tracked-out sans, ivory color.
- Card row 1: "IMAGE" (small sans label) + "₹9" (large serif tabular numeral, gold) + "per generation" (micro caption).
- Card row 2: "VIDEO" (small sans label) + "₹99" (large serif tabular numeral, gold) + "per generation" (micro caption).
- Below card: "This festive season." (small, ivory, centered).
- Proof strip: "GPT · Claude · Gemini · India-first voice — prepaid rupees, one GST invoice." (micro sans, low-emphasis).
- Bottom CTA: "Talk to us · getaight.ai" (sans, gold or ivory, clearly separated by whitespace).

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
All copy, the "aight ." logotype, the ₹9 / ₹99 numerals, qualifiers, tagline, proof strip and CTA are set as exact vector typography in post-production — not left to the generative model — to guarantee correct currency symbols, spelling, and brand-accurate wording sourced only from getaight.ai. Grid, margins, and contrast ratios are also enforced deterministically at export.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static single-image poster deliverable, no audio/motion/edit required.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: AI renders garbled/incorrect currency symbols or numerals → mitigated by keeping all text out of the generative layer and applying exact vector type afterward.
- Risk: poster reads as discount retail flyer → mitigated by strict palette lock (no red/orange sale colors, no starbursts/badges/percent-off graphics), and by anchoring festivity to a single diya + warm glow rather than decorative clutter.
- Risk: price hierarchy lost among festive imagery → mitigated by making the ledger card and its two numerals the single largest visual element on the canvas; all festive/brand elements are smaller and quieter.
- Risk: brand inaccuracy → all claims (tagline, models, prepaid/GST language) sourced verbatim/paraphrased only from the getaight.ai snapshot, nothing invented.
- Risk: illegibility at thumbnail size → verified via large numeral scale, strong gold/charcoal contrast, generous negative space around the ledger card.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Exactly one poster, one concept, no alternates: ✅
- 4:5 aspect ratio: ✅
- Both ₹9 (image) and ₹99 (video) immediately understandable: ✅ (hero-scale ledger numerals)
- Feels like serious AI infrastructure company, not discount flyer: ✅ (palette, ledger/invoice metaphor, restrained festive cue)
- Typography and hierarchy do most of the work: ✅ (imagery limited to texture + one diya glow; all messaging carried by type system)
- No model/provider recommended: ✅ (none named in package)
