## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One premium, print/social-ready still image, 4:5 aspect ratio, high resolution (minimum 4000×5000px), for a new Indian sparkling mosambi (sweet lime) beverage. Single hero product shot, no variants delivered.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The client needs a launch-grade hero image for a new category-defining drink, targeted at urban Indian 22–35-year-olds who associate "Indian-ness" with confidence and modern design fluency, not folklore. The image must do three things simultaneously: (1) make the product instantly, unmistakably the hero, (2) signal premium beverage codes (craft tonic / artisanal soda / boutique sparkling water aesthetics) rather than mass-market fizzy-drink codes, (3) retain just enough mosambi-fruit recognizability that the flavor promise is legible at a glance — without leaning on turbans, marigolds, rangoli, temples, or other stereotypical "Indian" visual shorthand.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Mosambi, reimagined as a modern ritual." The ad treats mosambi not as a nostalgic roadside-juice fruit but as a premium botanical ingredient — photographed the way a craft tonic or fine spirit brand would photograph citrus. A single elegant bottle stands as the unquestioned hero, accompanied only by one halved mosambi and suspended water droplets/bubbles, set against a quiet, softly lit gradient backdrop. Cultural contemporariness comes through restraint, materiality (stone, brass, frosted glass) and typographic confidence — not iconography.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary: The bottle (product form + brand name) — largest, sharpest, most lit element, center-right on a lower rule-of-thirds point.
2. Secondary: The mosambi half (fruit cue) — foreground, slightly soft-focus, confirms flavor without competing with the bottle.
3. Tertiary: Condensation, rising bubbles, light bloom — sensory cues of "refreshing" and "sparkling."
4. Background: Minimal gradient + material surface — establishes "premium" tier, carries zero competing information.
No text overlay, tagline, or logo lockup beyond the on-bottle label itself; the image must work as a clean hero asset that other layouts can add copy to later.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: warm cream-to-charcoal-olive gradient background; bottle in frosted sage/chartreuse glass; brass/gold foil cap and neck band; mosambi flesh in pale yellow-green with visible juicy segments.
- Materials: frosted glass, brushed brass, honed travertine or warm stone surface — signals craft/premium, not plastic.
- Lighting: single soft key light from upper-left (softbox quality), gentle rim light from behind-right to separate bottle from background, controlled specular highlights on glass and droplets — studio beverage-photography lighting, not flat e-commerce lighting.
- Composition: bottle occupies ~55–60% of frame height, positioned right-of-center; mosambi half and a few droplets/bubbles in lower-left third; generous negative space upper-left for potential later copy placement (kept empty in this deliverable).
- Mood: calm, confident, adult, tactile — closer to a craft tonic/artisanal soda campaign than a soft-drink commercial.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Step 1 — Generate base scene: bottle, mosambi, surface, background, lighting, bubbles/droplets, with label area left as a clean, distraction-free zone with plausible but non-final markings (to avoid garbled text from the generative model).
Step 2 — Composite: place a separately designed, pixel-precise vector label (brand wordmark, tagline, minimal mark) onto the bottle in post-production, matched to the bottle's perspective, lighting, and glass refraction using layer-based compositing (curvature warp + highlight/shadow pass).
Step 3 — Color grade: unify highlights/shadows, ensure bottle remains highest local contrast + sharpest focus point in the frame.
Step 4 — Final crop/export at 4:5.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final image-generation prompt (base scene, pre-label-compositing):

"Ultra-premium studio product photography, 4:5 vertical composition. A tall, slender frosted sage-green glass bottle of sparkling mosambi (Indian sweet lime) beverage stands right-of-center on a honed warm travertine surface. The bottle has a brushed brass cap and a thin brass neck band, with a clean, minimal, unlabeled or softly blurred placeholder label area on the body (no legible text). Beside it in the lower-left foreground, one mosambi (sweet lime) cut in half reveals pale yellow-green juicy segments, resting on the same stone surface with a few natural water droplets. Fine effervescent bubbles rise gently through the bottle's liquid and hover faintly in the air near the neck, frozen mid-motion. Condensation droplets bead naturally on the glass. Background is a soft, seamless gradient from warm cream at the top to deep charcoal-olive at the bottom, softly lit studio backdrop, no props, no people, no hands, no text, no logos other than the bottle's own blank label zone. Lighting: single soft key light upper-left, subtle rim light from behind-right creating clean glass highlights and soft shadow falloff onto the stone surface. Mood: sophisticated, refreshing, contemporary, adult, editorial beverage-campaign quality, comparable to premium craft tonic or artisanal sparkling water advertising — not a mass-market soft drink. Sharp focus on bottle and cap, gentle shallow depth of field on mosambi half and background. No celebrity, no human figures, no clichéd Indian iconography (no marigolds, no rangoli, no temples, no turbans, no Taj Mahal, no paisley). High-resolution, clean commercial product photography, physically accurate reflections and refractions through glass."

Negative/avoid list to include in the same generation call: "no plastic bottle look, no bright primary-color soda branding, no cartoon fruit splash graphics, no extra hands or fingers, no distorted or garbled text, no busy background, no multiple bottles, no low-quality stock-photo lighting, no oversaturated colors, no visible brand logos other than blank label zone."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Final brand wordmark, tagline, and any mark/logo must be built as a vector asset outside the generative model and composited onto the bottle in post-production, to guarantee typographic accuracy (generative models are unreliable for crisp small text).
- Label graphic spec (deterministic): brand name set in a modern, confident sans-serif (e.g., a geometric grotesque), small tagline "Sparkling Mosambi" beneath it, one restrained graphic mark (a single abstracted citrus-slice line-icon or minimal geometric motif — not a literal fruit cartoon), color: brass foil or deep olive ink on a frosted/pale-sage label field.
- This compositing pass is treated as a deterministic production step, not a generative one.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — this is a single static image deliverable, no audio or edit timeline required.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: generative text/logo distortion → mitigated by leaving label area blank/placeholder in generation and compositing final vector label in post.
- Risk: image drifting toward "cheap soda" codes (bright primary colors, plastic sheen, cartoon splash) → explicitly excluded via negative prompt and material choices (frosted glass, brass, stone).
- Risk: cliché "Indian" iconography creeping in → explicitly excluded via negative prompt; cultural contemporariness carried instead through material/typographic restraint.
- Risk: mosambi becoming unrecognizable → mitigated by including one clearly cut, visibly juicy mosambi half in sharp-enough focus in foreground.
- Risk: product not reading as hero → mitigated by compositional dominance (largest, sharpest, most lit object), rule-of-thirds placement, and empty negative space elsewhere.
- Risk: stray human hands/figures appearing → explicitly excluded via negative prompt.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: satisfied.
- Premium, refreshing, sophisticated, culturally contemporary while retaining mosambi familiarity: satisfied via material/lighting choices + visible mosambi half.
- Avoids clichéd Indian visual shorthand: satisfied, explicitly excluded in prompt.
- Avoids cheap soft-drink look: satisfied via glass/brass/stone materiality and negative prompt exclusions.
- Product is unmistakable hero: satisfied via composition, scale, focus, lighting hierarchy.
- No celebrity: satisfied, no human figures at all.
- No external website: satisfied, no such element used or referenced anywhere in the package.

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
- One (1) premium advertising still image, aspect ratio 4:5, delivered at 4000×5000 px (300 dpi equivalent), RGB, retouched to print/social ad quality.
- Clean top-third negative space reserved for brand logo/tagline to be composited in post (not baked into the generative output).

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The client is launching a modern Indian sparkling beverage (mosambi + sparkling water) targeting urban 22–35 year-olds who associate "premium" with restraint, texture, and craft rather than logos or heritage motifs. The image must instantly read as a serious, adult beverage-brand product shot — closer to a boutique tonic/craft-soda or premium cold-pressed juice campaign than a mass-market soft drink — while the fruit itself (mosambi) remains visually identifiable so the flavor promise isn't lost. No stereotypical "Indian-ness" (no paisley, henna, marigolds, elephants, flags, saris, temples). No celebrity, no third-party branding, no external references.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Quiet Fizz" — a single frozen moment of understated luxury: a slim glass bottle of the drink standing on warm travertine stone, condensation beading on the glass, a halved mosambi resting beside it with visible juicy flesh, and a filled glass mid-fizz catching soft window light. The mood is that of a design-forward café or a minimalist kitchen counter in a contemporary Indian home — natural materials (stone, linen, brass, matte ceramic) replace clichéd cultural signifiers, doing the cultural work through material and color sensibility rather than iconography. The mosambi itself becomes the only "Indian" cue needed — familiar, unforced, and appetizing.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Product bottle (hero, largest, sharpest focus, brightest highlight) — communicates "premium sparkling mosambi drink."
2. Halved mosambi fruit + juice glass — communicates flavor authenticity and refreshment.
3. Condensation, bubbles, light — communicate coldness/carbonation/craft.
4. Negative space top-third — reserved for wordmark/tagline (added later, not generated).
5. Background props (stone, linen, brass) — support premium/contemporary mood, never compete with product.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: warm stone beige/travertine background, muted terracotta shadows, mosambi's pale green-yellow flesh as the accent "pop" color, brushed brass/copper for warm metallic accents, off-white linen texture. No neon, no primary red/blue soda colors.
- Materials: honed stone slab surface, linen napkin with visible weave, brushed brass small dish or spoon, matte-finish glass bottle (not glossy plastic-shiny), thin crown cap or cork-topped closure in matte bronze.
- Bottle design: tall slim glass silhouette (mineral-water proportions, not cola-bottle curves), subtle pale sage-tinted glass, minimal wraparound label covering roughly the middle third, thin embossed line-art of a mosambi leaf/branch as the only decorative motif, small clean wordmark space (left blank/placeholder for logo lock-up).
- Lighting: single soft directional key light (simulating window light) from upper left, gentle rim light upper right to separate bottle edge from background, soft natural shadow falling right, no harsh studio glare, no theatrical colored gels.
- Composition: vertical 4:5 frame; bottle placed on right-of-center vertical third, filling ~65–70% of frame height; glass with drink positioned lower-left, slightly out of crisp focus (shallow depth via longer lens compression); halved mosambi fruit in the immediate foreground bottom edge, partially cropped for intimacy; top third left largely negative/soft-focus stone texture for logo placement.
- Depth of field: product bottle tack-sharp, foreground fruit slightly soft, background very soft bokeh — simulating macro/product lens (~100mm equivalent, f/6.3–f/8).

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
- Style reference: contemporary editorial beverage/product photography (think boutique tonic or artisanal soda campaigns), not soft-drink advertising, not food-blog flat-lay.
- Camera behavior: product-photography macro lens simulation, eye-level to slightly elevated angle (~15° above horizontal), tripod-stable framing, no wide-angle distortion.
- Lighting behavior: single large soft key (softbox/window simulation) camera-left, subtle bounce fill camera-right, controlled specular highlight on bottle shoulder and cap only.
- Styling detail: condensation droplets realistic and physically consistent with light direction; bubbles in glass fine and natural (not cartoon-large); mosambi rendered anatomically correct — larger and more oblate than a lime, pale yellow-green mottled skin, pithy white interior, pale straw-yellow juicy segments (must not look like an orange or lemon).
- Color grade: warm neutral grade, slightly desaturated background, saturation boost reserved only for the mosambi flesh and drink liquid to keep eye on product.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final prompt (image generation):

"Premium advertising product photograph, vertical 4:5 composition, ultra-high detail. A tall slim matte pale-sage glass bottle of a modern sparkling mosambi (sweet lime) drink stands on a honed warm travertine stone surface, condensation droplets beading realistically on the glass, catching soft directional window light from upper left with a subtle rim light on the right edge. The bottle has a minimal label covering the middle third with a delicate embossed line-art motif of a mosambi leaf and branch, and blank clean space reserved for a small wordmark (no readable text, no logo). Beside it, slightly softer focus, a short glass filled with the pale golden-green sparkling drink, fine natural bubbles rising, condensation on the glass. In the immediate foreground, a halved mosambi (sweet lime) fruit, anatomically correct — thick pale yellow-green mottled skin, white pith, juicy pale straw-colored segments glistening — partially cropped at frame edge. Background is a softly blurred warm stone and linen surface in muted terracotta and beige tones, with a small brushed brass dish or spoon slightly out of focus for material richness. Composition: bottle on right third, filling roughly two-thirds of frame height, tack-sharp focus on bottle, shallow depth of field, soft bokeh background, generous negative space in upper third for later logo placement. Lighting: soft single-source natural key light, gentle shadow falling right, no harsh glare, no colored gels. Mood: sophisticated, contemporary, refreshing, adult beverage brand aesthetic — comparable to premium artisanal tonic or cold-pressed juice advertising, NOT a mass-market soda. Color palette: warm stone beige, muted terracotta, brushed brass, pale sage glass, golden-green citrus tones. Photorealistic, editorial commercial photography, 100mm macro-style lens compression, f/7 depth of field, clean premium finish, no clutter, no extra bottles, no people, no hands, no text, no logos, no cultural iconography."

Negative prompt:
"paisley, henna, marigold garlands, elephant, temple, sari, Indian flag, Taj Mahal, mango, lemon, lime confusion, orange fruit substituted for mosambi, plastic bottle sheen, glossy soda-can aesthetic, neon colors, cartoon bubbles, oversized bubbles, exaggerated splash, celebrity, human hands, human face, multiple duplicate bottles, blurry label smear, distorted text, gibberish typography, cluttered background, harsh flash, overexposed highlights, low-detail render, plastic-looking condensation, unrealistic reflections, watermark."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Actual brand wordmark, logo mark, and any on-bottle typography must NOT be relied upon from the generative model (text rendering is unreliable). These will be designed as vector assets and composited in post-production onto the pre-reserved blank label area and top-third negative space.
- Final tagline/typography placement, kerning, and brand color-lock (if a specific Pantone/brand palette exists) to be applied deterministically in post via a design tool, not regenerated.
- Any regulatory text (net quantity, FSSAI mark, nutritional info) must be added as a deterministic overlay, never generated.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable only.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Mosambi misidentification risk (rendering as lemon/lime/orange): explicitly described fruit anatomy and color in prompt; reject outputs where fruit reads as citrus-orange or lime-green lime.
- Cheap-soda look risk: explicitly excluded neon palette, glossy plastic, cartoon bubbles, oversaturated primary colors via negative prompt and material specification (matte glass, stone, brass).
- Cultural cliché risk: explicit negative-prompt exclusion list (paisley, henna, marigold, elephant, flag, temple) plus positive direction toward minimal contemporary materials.
- Text/logo garbling risk: no text requested in generation; all typography deferred to deterministic post-production layer.
- Composition risk (product not hero): bottle mandated as largest, sharpest, most central-weighted element; reject any output where fruit, glass, or props visually outcompete the bottle in focus or scale.
- Realism risk (condensation/bubbles): specified physically consistent droplet and bubble behavior; reject exaggerated splash-frozen-in-air renders that look like soda-commercial CGI.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio image: satisfied.
- Premium, refreshing, sophisticated, culturally contemporary feel: satisfied via material/lighting/color system.
- Retains familiarity of mosambi: satisfied via accurate fruit rendering as key visual anchor.
- Avoids clichéd "Indian" visual shorthand: satisfied via explicit exclusion list and material-led design language.
- Avoids cheap soft-drink look: satisfied via matte glass, muted palette, editorial lighting, no neon/plastic cues.
- Product is unmistakable hero: satisfied via compositional dominance, sharpest focus, largest scale.
- No celebrity: satisfied, no human figures included at all.
- No external website reference: satisfied, no third-party site or brand referenced.

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One premium, print/social-ready advertising still image, 4:5 aspect ratio (e.g., 1600×2000 px minimum, scalable to 4000×5000 px for print), full colour, hero product photography style. Single final image only.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
Launch-style hero shot for a new Indian mosambi + sparkling water beverage, targeting urban 22–35 year-olds who associate themselves with contemporary design, wellness, and craft beverage culture rather than mass-market soft drinks. The image must read as premium and modern first, "Indian" second — through ingredient authenticity and material quality rather than décor, motifs, or costume signalling. Product recognisability and desirability are the primary success metrics.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Mosambi, elevated." A single slim aluminium can stands centre-stage in a moody, softly lit still-life set — caught mid-moment as a citrus-bright splash of carbonated water arcs around it, with a halved mosambi and glistening droplets grounding the drink in real fruit. The palette (muted charcoal, stone, brass, and the drink's own chartreuse-gold) does the cultural work: warm, sun-washed, orchard-adjacent, but styled like a design-object, not a soda ad. No people, no landmarks, no folk motifs — just craft, light, and fruit.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Product (can) — unmistakable hero, largest, sharpest, centre-weighted.
2. Freshness cue — splash + citrus slice + droplets, directly interacting with the can.
3. Mood/positioning — lighting, background gradient, surface reflection (premium, calm, adult).
4. Brand mark — small, confident wordmark on can body only (no additional on-image text/copy).
No headline, no price, no logo lockup outside the can — keeps the product as the only communicator.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Format: vertical 4:5, product occupying middle third, slight low-angle hero framing.
- Palette: deep charcoal-to-soft-moss gradient background; can body in warm chartreuse-to-ivory gradient with a thin brushed-brass accent ring; no red/orange soda cues.
- Materials: matte-finish aluminium can (not glossy soda-can plastic look), wet stone or dark honed-marble surface for reflection.
- Props: one halved mosambi (cut face visible, pale green-gold flesh), 2–3 loose citrus segments, scattered water droplets, a controlled splash arc with visible fine bubbles — no overflowing "fizz mess."
- Lighting: soft key light top-left (large softbox feel), subtle warm rim light right edge separating product from background, gentle fill to retain shadow detail; no harsh commercial soda flash.
- Depth: shallow depth of field, background softly defocused with faint warm bokeh (suggesting orchard light without literal leaves/trees dominating).
- Explicitly avoided: turmeric-hand shots, marigolds, paisley, saris, monuments, auto-rickshaws, neon Bollywood palettes, plastic-bottle soda tropes, cluttered ice-and-splash chaos.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Generate base composition via single text-to-image prompt (below) at 4:5, high detail, photographic realism.
2. Select best candidate for: can geometry accuracy, splash realism, correct 4:5 framing, absence of stray text artefacts.
3. Post-production pass (deterministic, non-generative): clean up label surface, add vector-based brand wordmark/logo, colour-grade for premium consistency, refine droplet highlights, confirm crop to exact 4:5.
4. Final QC against hard constraints before delivery.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final prompt (image, one shot):

"Ultra-premium studio product photograph, vertical 4:5 composition, a single slim matte-finish aluminium beverage can centred slightly below frame middle, label surface left deliberately clean/blank (no readable text or logos), can body colour a soft chartreuse-to-pale-ivory gradient with a thin brushed brass accent ring near the top. The can sits on a dark honed-marble wet surface with a subtle mirror reflection. Around the can, a dynamic but controlled arc of sparkling water splash frozen mid-motion with fine, realistic bubbles, alongside a freshly halved mosambi (sweet lime) showing pale green-gold flesh, two loose citrus segments, and scattered natural water droplets on the can and surface. Background is a smooth dark charcoal-to-soft-moss gradient with soft warm defocused bokeh, no landmarks, no people, no folk motifs, no plastic soda-bottle aesthetic. Lighting: large soft key light from top-left, subtle warm rim light on right edge separating product from background, gentle fill light preserving shadow detail, cinematic but clean commercial studio lighting. Shot on a macro/product lens look, shallow depth of field, background softly out of focus, product in crisp sharp focus. Mood: sophisticated, modern, adult, refreshing, minimal, high-end beverage advertising, no text anywhere in image, no watermark, no extra props, no ice cubes, no straws."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Brand wordmark/logo on can: added in post as a vector overlay (not generated) to guarantee crisp, correct typography — placed on the pre-left-blank label zone.
- Final colour grade and contrast pass: standard non-generative post (LUT/curves) for cross-platform consistency.
- Exact 4:5 crop/canvas confirmation and export at required resolutions.
- Any nutritional/legal disclaimer text (if required by client) added as deterministic overlay, kept off primary visual zone.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable only.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: AI renders can label with garbled text → Mitigated by keeping label blank in generation prompt; logo added deterministically in post.
- Risk: Composition drifts toward generic red/orange soda-can look → Explicit palette and "no plastic soda-bottle aesthetic" constraint in prompt.
- Risk: Cultural clichés creeping in (mango leaves, marigold, monuments) → Explicitly excluded in prompt; background kept abstract/bokeh only.
- Risk: Splash/bubbles look chaotic or cheap → Prompt specifies "controlled," "fine bubbles," avoids "overflowing" language.
- Risk: Wrong aspect ratio or off-centre crop → Framing and canvas explicitly locked to 4:5 with product centred in prompt and QC step.
- Risk: Product not clearly the hero (props overpower) → Hierarchy enforced via prompt ordering, size relationships, and sharp-focus/shallow-DOF direction.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: ✅ specified in canvas and prompt.
- Product is unmistakable hero: ✅ centred, sharp, largest element.
- No celebrity: ✅ no people in image.
- No external website: ✅ none referenced or shown.
- Avoids clichéd "Indian" visual shorthand: ✅ explicitly excluded in prompt and visual system.
- Avoids cheap soft-drink look: ✅ matte can, muted palette, controlled splash, premium lighting.
- Retains mosambi familiarity: ✅ visible halved fruit and segments, characteristic pale green-gold color cues.

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One premium, commercially usable e-commerce hero image, 4:5 aspect ratio (e.g., 1600×2000 px minimum, delivered as high-res sRGB JPEG/PNG plus a layered master), depicting the Aster Meridian 38 watch as a single, photorealistic still-life product shot.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The image must function as a primary product-page hero: it needs to sell craftsmanship and material quality at a glance, hold up under zoom/crop for a PDP gallery, and read cleanly on a light or dark storefront background. The customer wants a grounded, tactile, "real object" feel rather than a synthetic floating-CGI look, and demands strict fidelity to fixed product geometry — no reinterpretation of case size, dial layout, hands, crystal, or strap.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A intimate, studio-lit tabletop still life: the watch rests naturally on a dark walnut surface with its dark brown leather strap coiled loosely beside/behind the case, photographed at a low three-quarter hero angle that lets a single large soft key light rake across the dial to ignite the blue sunburst pattern and pick out the polished bevels, dauphine hands, and dome of the sapphire crystal. This is presented as an aspirational, editorial-grade product photograph — the kind seen in luxury watch catalogs — not a rendered CGI floating object, because the surface contact, real shadow falloff, and material specularity are what sell craftsmanship and desirability believably.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary: dial and crystal (craftsmanship, sunburst finish, precision of hands/markers) — largest, sharpest, most lit area, positioned in upper-middle third.
2. Secondary: case material (brushed steel finish, polished bevel edge, crown) — visible along the case flank, catching a distinct rim light.
3. Tertiary: strap material and stitching (leather grain, tone, craftsmanship of tailoring) — lower third, softly lit, leading the eye out of frame.
4. Supporting: clean negative space at top ~15% of frame and soft background falloff, preserving room for a retailer to later overlay logo/price without a text layer being baked into this deliverable.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Aspect ratio: 4:5, vertical.
- Palette: deep sunburst blue (dial) + brushed cool steel + warm dark walnut brown (strap + surface) + near-black charcoal gradient background — a restrained, premium 3-tone palette.
- Lighting: single large soft key (softbox equivalent, ~45° top-left), gentle fill from camera-right to open case shadow detail, small hard kicker to define bezel edge, background lit separately and slightly dimmer to create a soft vignette.
- Camera language: macro-adjacent product photography — 100mm macro equivalent, low tripod-height three-quarter angle (~35–40° above horizontal), shallow-to-moderate depth of field (dial and nearest hands tack sharp, strap coil and background softly falling off), no wide-angle distortion of the case.
- Surface/props: single dark walnut wood surface with soft reflection, no clutter, no extra props, no hands/fingers holding the watch.
- Mood: quiet luxury, tactile, confident — not flashy, no lens flares, no exaggerated CGI gloss.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Generate base image from the single prompt below at high resolution, framed natively in 4:5.
2. Generate 4–6 candidate variants (same prompt, seed variation only) to allow selection of the version with most accurate hand geometry, marker spacing, and crown placement.
3. Select the candidate with: correct hand count/shape (dauphine, silver, two hands only — hour and minute, plus seconds hand if present must be thin/simple and not mistaken for a date complication), no visible date window, symmetric baton markers, correctly domed (not flat) crystal.
4. Non-generative retouch pass (deterministic, manual/Photoshop-style): 
   - Verify and, if needed, locally correct dial symmetry and marker alignment.
   - Clean any sensor-noise/artifact specks on the crystal or dial.
   - Calibrate blue channel so the dial reads as a true deep sunburst blue (not teal/purple) under both display and print color profiles.
   - Sharpen dial/hands selectively; keep strap and background at softer sharpness to preserve depth hierarchy.
   - Confirm strap is dark brown leather with visible grain/stitching, not black or reddish.
5. Final color grade: subtle contrast lift, gentle vignette reinforcement, export sRGB with embedded profile for e-commerce use.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final image prompt (execute at 4:5, e.g., 1600x2000):

"Ultra-detailed, photorealistic luxury product photograph of a men's mechanical wristwatch, the 'Aster Meridian 38', shot as a still life on a dark polished walnut wood surface, low three-quarter hero angle roughly 35-40 degrees above horizontal. The watch: 38mm brushed stainless-steel round case with a distinct polished bevel edge, fine directional brushed-metal texture on the case flank, visible crown at 3 o'clock. Dial: deep blue sunburst finish with radial brushed texture catching light, silver polished baton hour markers evenly spaced, silver polished dauphine hour and minute hands with sharp faceted edges catching a bright specular highlight, no date window, no other complications, clean symmetrical dial layout. Crystal: clearly domed sapphire crystal with subtle barrel-shaped optical distortion of the dial edges and a soft, controlled reflection of a large studio softbox on its upper curve, not obscuring the dial. Strap: dark brown genuine leather strap, fine natural grain, visible top-stitching, strap coiled loosely and draped naturally beside and slightly behind the case, resting on the wood surface. Lighting: one large soft key light from upper left raking across the dial to reveal sunburst texture, gentle fill light from the right to open shadow detail on the case, a small controlled kicker light defining the case bezel, separate dim background light creating a soft charcoal-to-black gradient vignette. Camera: 100mm macro-equivalent lens, shallow-to-moderate depth of field, dial and nearest hands in critical sharp focus, strap coil and background surface softly falling off. Composition: watch positioned center-lower two-thirds of frame, dial slightly left of center, generous clean negative space in the top 15 percent of the frame, no text, no logos, no watermark, no human hands or fingers, no props other than the watch, strap, and wood surface. Style: quiet luxury editorial watch catalog photography, tactile, true-to-material, no CGI plastic sheen, no exaggerated gloss, no lens flare, no floating product, natural contact shadow beneath case and strap. Vertical 4:5 aspect ratio."

Negative/exclusion guidance (apply as negative prompt or generation constraints where supported):
"no date window, no additional dial complications, no chronograph subdials, no visible brand logo or text, no watermark, no floating/levitating watch, no glossy plastic CGI look, no distorted or oversized case proportions, no mismatched or asymmetric hour markers, no flat crystal, no black or red strap, no fingers or hands in frame, no cluttered background props, no harsh unmotivated lens flare, no double crown, no blurry dial."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Final color calibration/grading pass (blue-channel correction, contrast, vignette) performed as a deterministic post-process, not re-generated.
- Manual pixel-level correction of any dial/marker asymmetry or artifact via standard retouching (non-generative inpainting/cloning) rather than re-prompting, to guarantee fixed geometry is preserved exactly.
- Export/color-profile management (sRGB embed) is a deterministic technical step.
- No overlaid text, logo, or price graphics are part of this deliverable (kept clean for downstream commercial use).

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable, no audio or edit timeline required.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Primary risk: AI-generated hands/markers drifting into an unintended "date window" or extra subdial → mitigated by explicit negative prompt and mandatory manual verification step before approval.
- Risk: crystal rendered flat instead of domed, losing the sapphire-dome cue → explicitly specified with distortion/reflection cues and checked at selection stage.
- Risk: dial color skewing teal/purple instead of "deep blue sunburst" → corrected via deterministic color-calibration pass.
- Risk: image drifting into generic floating-CGI product-render aesthetic → explicitly forbidden in prompt; composition anchored with real surface contact and shadow.
- Risk: strap color/material misread (black, suede, or synthetic look) → explicitly reinforced ("dark brown genuine leather... natural grain... top-stitching") and checked in QA pass.
- Risk: over-cluttered composition reducing e-commerce usability → single surface, no extra props, controlled negative space enforced.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 38mm brushed stainless-steel case: specified explicitly. ✔
- Deep blue sunburst dial: specified explicitly, with color-calibration safeguard. ✔
- Silver baton hour markers: specified explicitly. ✔
- Silver dauphine hands: specified explicitly, faceted detail called out. ✔
- No date window: explicitly stated in both main prompt and negative prompt. ✔
- Domed sapphire crystal: explicitly specified with distortion/reflection cues. ✔
- Dark brown leather strap: explicitly specified with grain/stitching detail. ✔
- 4:5 aspect ratio: explicitly specified and locked in output dimensions. ✔
- Craftsmanship/dial detail/material quality/desirability communicated: addressed via lighting, macro detail focus, and composition hierarchy. ✔
- Avoid generic floating CGI: explicitly rejected in creative idea and negative prompt; grounded studio still-life chosen instead. ✔
- No external website used: confirmed, none referenced. ✔
- Single deliverable, no alternates presented: confirmed. ✔

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One premium 4:5 (1080×1350 px minimum, deliver at 4000×5000 px) static e-commerce hero image of the Aster Meridian 38 wristwatch. Single final image, no variants, print/web dual-purpose resolution, sRGB with a CMYK-safe color range for potential print catalog reuse.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The image's job is to sell desirability and perceived craftsmanship in a single frame on a product listing page, where shoppers scan fast and zoom into dial/case detail before purchase. Success = the shopper immediately reads "precision mechanical watch, quality materials" without needing copy, and the image survives close inspection (dial texture, hand shapes, crystal dome, strap grain) since e-commerce shoppers pixel-peep before buying a $ item like this. Constraint: this is a real product with fixed geometry — the image must look like an actual photograph of that specific watch, not a stylized or reinterpreted CGI object.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A tightly-composed three-quarter studio product shot — the watch resting at a slight angle on a dark, softly lit surface, strap curving naturally into frame — lit the way high-end watch catalogs (the kind used by real Swiss brands) light steel and sunburst dials: one raking key light to draw out the sunburst brushing on the dial and the brushed-steel case flanks, a controlled highlight arc on the domed sapphire crystal that reveals depth without blowing out the hour markers, and a soft gradient dark background that keeps all visual attention on the watch. No lifestyle props, no wrist, no floating CGI — just an honest, expensive-looking macro-leaning studio photograph.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary read (first 0.5s): "This is a serious mechanical watch" — silhouette, proportion, case-to-strap relationship.
2. Secondary read: dial craftsmanship — sunburst blue finish, silver baton markers and dauphine hands catching light, clean dial (no date window) signaling deliberate, uncluttered design.
3. Tertiary read: material quality — brushed steel texture on case flank/lugs, sapphire crystal dome causing a controlled light bend, leather grain on the strap.
4. Background/composition stays fully subordinate — dark, non-competing, providing negative space in the upper third usable for a logo/price overlay if the page template needs it, without that being the image's main content.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Framing: three-quarter front-left angle, watch face tilted ~20–25° toward camera, case slightly elevated on the far side so the dial's sunburst pattern catches a visible gradient sweep.
- Lens/optics language: macro-photography feel, ~100mm macro equivalent, f/7.1–f/8 so the whole watch face and near case edge are crisp while the strap tail and background fall into soft, shallow bokeh.
- Lighting: one large soft key light upper-left at ~45°, raking across the dial to reveal sunburst brushing; a narrow strip light/reflector on the right to trace a clean highlight along the case's brushed flank and crystal dome edge; soft fill from below to prevent the underside of the lugs and strap from going full black; a small rim light behind to separate the case silhouette from the background.
- Background: seamless dark charcoal-to-black gradient, warm-neutral, no texture, no props, no hands/wrist, no water/rocks/other generic CGI staging.
- Surface: watch rests on a dark walnut or matte black solid surface with a very subtle reflection of the case underside (not mirror-glossy, just enough to ground the object physically).
- Color grading: rich deep navy dial blues, cool brushed-steel silvers, warm dark-brown leather, neutral-to-slightly-warm background — contrast tuned so blue dial and silver hands are the brightest saturated elements.
- Composition/negative space: watch occupies center-to-lower two-thirds of frame; upper third kept darker/emptier for optional page overlay use without cropping the product.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Single AI-generated still image, generated at high resolution in 4:5 native aspect ratio (no post-crop from a square or 16:9 source, to avoid losing detail). One generation pass targeting photorealistic studio product photography, followed by light retouch-style post-processing (contrast/dodge-burn refinement, dust/spec cleanup, color calibration) treated as standard e-commerce retouching, not content alteration. No compositing of multiple watches or props; single coherent scene, single subject.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Final single generation prompt (image):

"Ultra-detailed, photorealistic studio product photograph of a luxury men's mechanical wristwatch, the 'Aster Meridian 38.' Exact product specs, must be rendered precisely and not altered: 38mm brushed stainless-steel round case with visible fine brushed-metal grain on the case flanks and lugs; deep blue sunburst dial with radial brushed sunburst pattern catching light; silver polished baton-style hour markers evenly spaced; silver dauphine-style hands (hour, minute, thin sweep second hand) polished and faceted; completely clean dial with no date window or date complication; a clear domed sapphire crystal over the dial showing subtle realistic curvature, light refraction and a controlled specular highlight arc without obscuring the hour markers; dark brown genuine leather strap with visible natural grain texture, stitched edges, curving naturally out of frame. Watch positioned at a three-quarter front-left angle, tilted about 20-25 degrees toward camera, resting on a dark walnut wood or matte black surface with a soft, subtle reflection beneath the case. Studio lighting: large soft key light from upper-left raking across the dial to reveal sunburst texture, narrow highlight/rim light along the right case edge and crystal dome, soft fill light to retain detail in shadow areas under the lugs and strap, gentle rim light separating the case from the background. Background: seamless dark charcoal-to-black gradient, no props, no hands, no wrist, no water or rocks, no text, no logos. Shot as if with a 100mm macro lens at f/8, tack-sharp focus on the dial and near case edge, soft gradual falloff toward the strap tail. Color grading: deep saturated navy-blue dial, cool brushed silver metal tones, warm dark-brown leather, neutral dark background. Composition: vertical 4:5 aspect ratio, watch centered in lower two-thirds of frame, subtle empty dark space in upper third. Style: premium Swiss watch catalog photography, honest material representation, high commercial polish, no stylization, no fantasy elements, no floating or CGI-glossy rendering artifacts."

Negative/avoidance direction to include in the same generation instruction: "no date window, no additional dial complications, no extra hands, no distorted case shape, no oversized or undersized case proportions, no plastic-looking crystal, no cartoonish reflections, no visible watch brand logos or text, no wrist or skin, no lifestyle background, no multiple watches."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
None required as separate layers — this is a single-shot generated hero image with no overlay text, no logo, no UI chrome specified by the brief. If the e-commerce template later requires logo/price/CTA overlay, the upper-third negative space is reserved deterministically in the composition for that non-generative overlay to be added in a separate templating step (not part of this image generation).

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable only.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Case size/proportion drift: explicitly lock 38mm round case proportions and standard lug-to-case ratio in prompt; reject outputs where case reads as oversized/diver-style or undersized/dress-thin beyond stated spec.
- Dial alteration risk: explicitly forbid date window and extra complications; reject any output adding a date cyclops, chronograph subdials, or extra hands.
- Hands/markers mismatch: explicitly specify baton markers + dauphine hands; reject Arabic numerals, diamond markers, or mismatched hand styles.
- Crystal misread: specify domed sapphire with controlled highlight; reject flat-looking crystal or one with a blown-out hotspot that hides markers.
- Strap material drift: explicitly lock dark brown leather with visible grain and stitching; reject metal bracelet, rubber strap, or wrong strap color.
- Generic floating-CGI drift: explicitly anchor watch to a physical surface with grounded soft reflection and studio lighting logic; reject weightless/floating renders, glossy toy-like CGI, or unrealistic environment reflections.
- Background overreach: reject busy, textured, or lifestyle backgrounds that compete with the product; enforce seamless dark gradient only.
- Text/logo hallucination: explicitly forbid rendered brand text/logo on dial or case since none was specified; reject any invented branding.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: specified and enforced in prompt and deliverable spec. ✔
- 38mm brushed stainless-steel case: explicitly specified. ✔
- Deep blue sunburst dial: explicitly specified. ✔
- Silver baton hour markers: explicitly specified. ✔
- Silver dauphine hands: explicitly specified. ✔
- No date window: explicitly forbidden in prompt and failure-prevention. ✔
- Domed sapphire crystal: explicitly specified with realistic refraction. ✔
- Dark brown leather strap: explicitly specified with grain/stitching detail. ✔
- Craftsmanship/dial detail/material quality/desirability communicated: addressed via lighting system, macro-level detail rendering, and composition hierarchy. ✔
- Product geometry not casually altered: locked via explicit spec list and rejection criteria. ✔
- No generic floating-product CGI: explicitly avoided via grounded studio-photo treatment; creative idea does not require floating CGI. ✔
- No external website: no web sources used or referenced. ✔

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One static, photoreal 4:5 (2000×2500 px, sRGB, JPEG/PNG master) e-commerce hero image of the Aster Meridian 38, suitable as the primary PDP (product detail page) image. Single frame, no variants.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer needs a commercially safe, high-trust hero shot: one that reads instantly as "premium mechanical watch," lets a shopper evaluate dial finish, case brushing, hand/marker style, crystal dome and strap material at a glance, and does so without inventing or omitting any spec (critically: no date window). "Avoid generic floating CGI" signals the customer wants tactile, photographic credibility over sterile render-on-white commerce shots — so the solution should look like real studio product photography, grounded on a surface, not a weightless 3D asset.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Quiet Craft" — a three-quarter studio macro shot of the watch resting on a dark honed-stone surface, strap softly coiled beside/under the case. A single sculpted soft-box light sweeps a controlled specular highlight across the brushed case and sunburst dial, revealing radial brushing, hand geometry and marker facets, while the leather strap's grain and stitching sit in gentle shadow-fall focus. This grounds the product physically (defeats "floating CGI" concern) while still isolating it enough for clean e-commerce use.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Watch as singular hero, dial and hands in critical sharp focus (craftsmanship + desirability).
2. Case brushing and bezel chamfer catching the key light (material quality).
3. Sunburst dial texture and silver baton markers/dauphine hands (dial detail, correctness of spec).
4. Domed sapphire crystal's subtle curved reflection (premium cue, no distortion of dial info).
5. Leather strap grain/stitching, softly resolved (secondary material story).
6. Neutral dark gradient background — supports, never competes.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: deep navy sunburst blue, warm dark-brown leather, cool steel silver, charcoal/graphite background.
- Angle: 3/4 hero angle, dial tilted ~20° toward camera, watch occupying 60–70% of frame height, positioned on a lower-third/rule-of-thirds axis with negative space above for page UI overlay.
- Lighting: one large soft key top-left at ~45°, soft fill opposite to prevent black-crush, thin rim light along case edge to separate from background, one controlled specular streak crossing the dial's sunburst grain.
- Lens/DOF: 90mm macro look, medium-shallow DOF — dial, hands, crystal and near-side case sharp; strap tail and far background ease into soft focus.
- Surface: dark honed slate/stone, very soft contact shadow, no distracting props, no text, no logos, no watermark.
- No date window, no added complications, no numerals — dial stays true to spec (baton markers, dauphine hands, plain sunburst blue).

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Treat as a single simulated studio photography setup rather than a floating render:
1. Ground the watch on a real-feeling surface with visible micro-contact shadow.
2. Light it like a photographed object (single key + fill + rim), not flat ambient CGI light.
3. Emphasize material physics: brushed metal anisotropic highlight, leather micro-grain, sapphire dome curvature with a subtle realistic reflection (not a flare).
4. Compose for commerce: centered-ish, breathing room, crop-safe for PDP thumbnails.
5. Post: fine local contrast on dial/case, controlled highlight rolloff on crystal, accurate blue calibration, gentle sharpening on markers/hands only, final crop locked to 4:5.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final image — one generation pass (upscale/retouch as needed, no compositing of separate elements required).

Positive prompt:
"Premium studio product photograph of a men's mechanical wristwatch, the Aster Meridian 38: 38mm round brushed stainless-steel case with a polished chamfered bezel edge, deep blue sunburst dial with fine radial brushed texture catching light, applied silver baton hour markers (no numerals), silver dauphine hour and minute hands plus a slim silver seconds hand, absolutely no date window or date aperture anywhere on the dial, domed sapphire crystal with a subtle realistic curved reflection, dark brown grained leather strap with visible stitching and natural creasing, strap gently coiled beside and partly beneath the case. Watch shown at a three-quarter angle, dial tilted about 20 degrees toward camera, resting on a dark charcoal honed-stone surface with a soft natural contact shadow. Single large soft key light from upper left producing a controlled specular highlight sweep across the brushed case and sunburst dial, subtle rim light along the case edge, gentle fill to avoid harsh shadows. Macro product photography look, 90mm lens perspective, medium-shallow depth of field with dial, hands and near case in critical focus, crystal reflection realistic and restrained, no lens flare artifacts. Background dark neutral graphite gradient, softly defocused, no props, no text, no logo, no watermark. Photorealistic, hyper-detailed textures — micro-brushed steel, leather grain, sunburst dial sheen — true-to-life deep blue dial color, neutral 5500K studio color temperature. Vertical 4:5 composition, watch filling roughly 60-70% of frame height, premium e-commerce hero image quality."

Negative prompt:
"no date window, no date aperture, no cyclops lens, no chronograph subdials, no extra complications, no rotating bezel markings, no digital or luminous glow effects, no numerals on dial, no sword hands, no skeleton hands, no incorrect hand shapes, no floating watch with no surface contact, no cartoonish or plastic CGI look, no oversaturated or neon blue, no added logo, no added text, no watermark, no border, no second watch, no distorted or oval case shape, no scratched or damaged strap, no visible brand markings beyond a plain dial."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Aspect ratio and canvas crop (4:5, 2000×2500) locked as a deterministic post-generation crop/export step, not left to the generator's framing alone.
- Final color calibration (dial blue value, neutral background gray) fixed via a deterministic grading pass to guarantee consistency with brand/product-fact color rather than model-to-model drift.
- No other non-generative assets are required (no compositing of a separately shot strap or dial).

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable (single static image). Post-production edit pass: spot-clean any artifacting, verify dial has zero date cutout, refine specular highlight on sunburst grain for legibility, light global contrast/clarity pass, crop-lock to 4:5, export web-optimized master plus a compressed PDP version.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
Key risks and mitigations:
- Model adds a date window/cyclops → explicit repeated negative prompt + final visual QA check on dial at 100% zoom before approval.
- Generic floating/CGI look → mitigated by surface contact, shadow, coiled strap, photographic lighting language instead of "render," "3D," "CGI" terms.
- Wrong hand style (sword/skeleton) or numerals → explicitly named and negative-prompted.
- Extra complications (subdials, rotating bezel scale) → explicitly excluded.
- Oversaturated/incorrect blue → color temperature and "true-to-life" language plus deterministic grading pass.
- Logo/watermark/text intrusion → explicit exclusion.
- Case proportions distorted (ellipse instead of round 38mm) → angle constrained to 20° tilt, round case explicitly stated.
QA checklist before delivery: dial free of date cutout; markers are batons only; hands are dauphine style (hour/minute) plus thin seconds hand; crystal shows dome curvature without obscuring dial; strap is dark brown leather with grain/stitching visible; background is neutral, uncluttered; crop is exactly 4:5.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: satisfied (2000×2500 lock).
- Single image deliverable: satisfied.
- Fixed product facts (38mm brushed steel case, deep blue sunburst dial, silver baton markers, silver dauphine hands, no date window, domed sapphire crystal, dark brown leather strap): all explicitly encoded in prompt and negative prompt.
- Craftsmanship/dial detail/material quality/desirability communicated: satisfied via lighting, macro focus, material rendering plan.
- Premium, commercially usable on a product page: satisfied via clean neutral background, centered composition, no clutter.
- Avoid generic floating CGI unless idea requires it: satisfied — grounded studio-photo treatment chosen instead.
- No external website: satisfied, none referenced or required.
