## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) still image, 4:5 vertical aspect ratio (e.g., 3200×4000 px final), premium e‑commerce hero/PDP-hero image for the "Aster Meridian 38" mechanical watch. Single deliverable, no variants.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The image must function as a primary product-page hero: it has to sell the watch on sight by making material quality, dial craftsmanship and wrist-desirability legible within a fraction of a second, while being clean and neutral enough to sit next to price, buy button and spec copy without visual competition. It must read as a real, physically credible object — not a rendered floating asset — because credibility of materials (steel, sapphire, leather, sunburst finish) is the entire commercial argument for a watch at this positioning. All stated product facts are treated as fixed and non-negotiable identity constraints, not stylistic suggestions.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A grounded, tabletop studio photograph aesthetic: the watch rests at a confident three-quarter angle on a dark, softly gradated surface, angled so the crown and dial catch a controlled raking light. The sunburst dial's radial brushing is caught mid-sweep (glinting arc across the blue, not flat), the sapphire dome shows a single believable curved highlight along its edge (proof of domed glass) without a reflection blob over the hour markers, the case's brushed finish shows directional grain with one bright specular spine along the bezel, and the strap curls naturally into soft shadow in the lower foreground, showing grain and stitching. Nothing floats, nothing is CGI-clean; it looks shot, not rendered.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary read (first glance): "This is a real, finely made watch" — silhouette, blue dial glow, steel gleam.
2. Secondary read (on dwell): dial detail — baton markers, dauphine hands, sunburst texture, absence of clutter (no date window, confirming the clean dial promise).
3. Tertiary read: material story — brushed steel case texture, domed sapphire curvature, leather strap grain/stitching, all confirming "craftsmanship" as the underlying brand claim.
4. No text, logo or price is baked into the image; it is a pure hero photograph meant to carry copy placed by the page template outside the image (safe margins reserved top and bottom).

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Camera framing: 3/4 view, watch head as clear visual hero (Ogilvy's product-as-hero principle: product occupies the visual and psychological center, no competing elements) — dial and case fill approx. 55–65% of frame height, strap trailing into soft lower-frame shadow, generous negative space above for page headline overlay.
- Angle: watch tilted ~30–35° toward camera, crown pointing lower-right, so the sunburst dial's radial grain catches a distinct sweep of light rather than reading flat.
- Background: seamless dark charcoal-to-near-black gradient, cool-neutral, subtly darker at edges (vignette) to isolate the product without looking like a void; no props, no hands, no wrist.
- Surface: dark matte-satin tabletop with a very soft, short, believable contact shadow/reflection gradient directly under the case — enough to ground the object physically, not a mirror-clone reflection.
- Color palette: deep blue dial as the single chromatic accent against neutral charcoal/steel/brown; strap brown kept warm but not competing with the cool blue-steel dominant.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Lighting logic (grounded in classic reflective-surface product lighting principle — "the elegant compromise": one large soft key does double duty, since a polished/metallic surface only responds to light within its narrow reflection "family of angles" while the diffuse background responds to the whole source):
- Key: one large soft overhead-to-front softbox, positioned so a portion of it sits inside the reflection family of the bezel and hands (making steel and dauphine hands bright and defined) while its broader spread lifts the dial and case body softly.
- Fill: low-contrast fill card camera-left to hold shadow detail in the brushed case flank and strap without flattening the sunburst texture.
- Negative fill / gobo: a black flag opposite the key, positioned to place a dark, edge-defining reflection along the case's far rim and bezel edge — this is what separates the steel silhouette from the dark background and reads as "premium," rather than the metal blending into a flat grey.
- Crystal control: light and camera geometry set so the domed sapphire's direct reflection falls as a slim curved arc near the crystal's edge/bevel, deliberately kept off the hour markers and hand-cluster so dial legibility is not sacrificed for material proof.
- Strap: soft secondary light or bounce, low angle, to skim across the leather grain and stitching without hot spots.
- Distance/size control used as the fine-tuning tool: source size and distance are set so the case reads as a rich, deep steel tone (not blown white, not muddy grey) while the background stays a clean dark gradient independently.

Post-checks before accepting a frame (derived from standard polished-metal QA logic):
- Does the steel show real tonal range (dark-to-bright sweep across the brushed grain), or has it collapsed into a flat mid-grey card look? If flat, the piece will look like plastic — reject.
- Does the case's silhouette separate cleanly from the background at every edge (no edge merging into a same-tone backdrop)?
- Is there any hard-edged rectangular light-source reflection sitting artificially in the crystal or bezel? If yes, it must be softened/curved to look like a natural bevel highlight, not a studio-light cutout.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final generation prompt (image):

"Premium studio product photograph of a men's mechanical wristwatch, shot for a luxury e-commerce hero image, 4:5 vertical composition. The watch: 38 mm brushed stainless-steel round case with a visible fine directional brushed-metal grain and one crisp specular highlight arc along the polished bezel edge; deep blue sunburst dial with a clearly visible radial brushing pattern catching light in a directional sweep; silver baton hour markers evenly spaced, applied and slightly raised, catching bright highlights; silver dauphine (faceted triangular) hour and minute hands, crisp and reflective; a plain dial with no date window and no additional dial text/complications; a gently domed sapphire crystal with a single soft curved highlight along its edge/bevel, crystal otherwise clear so all dial elements stay sharp and legible; dark brown genuine leather strap with visible fine grain texture and neat contrast stitching, curling naturally out of frame in soft shadow in the lower foreground. Watch posed at a three-quarter angle, crown lower-right, resting on a dark matte tabletop surface with a soft realistic contact shadow, against a seamless dark charcoal-to-near-black gradient background with subtle vignette, no props, no hands, no wrist, no other objects. Lighting: large soft key light producing controlled, believable specular highlights on steel and dauphine hands, gentle fill retaining shadow detail in the case flank, dark negative-fill edge along the far rim of the case separating it from the background, natural soft light on the leather strap. Photographic realism: macro-capable product photography look, shallow-to-moderate depth of field keeping the entire dial and case in sharp focus with soft falloff on the strap tail and background, true-to-life materials, no CGI sheen, no plastic-looking metal, no HDR halo, no overexposed blown highlights, no visible studio light shapes reflected as hard rectangles, no logos, no text, no watermark, no wrist, no hands, no extra hour markers, no date window, no additional dial complications, no second hand unless naturally present, correct round case geometry, no case shape distortion, no melted or warped crystal, no doubled hands, no asymmetric markers, ultra-detailed, commercial product photography, 4:5 aspect ratio, vertical framing with headroom above the watch for page copy."

Construction plan:
1. Establish product identity lock first (case shape, dial layout, hand style, absence of date window, strap material/color) as non-negotiable elements repeated at both the start and the negative-constraint end of the prompt.
2. Generate base composition focusing on framing/angle/lighting per Visual System above.
3. Evaluate against the three polished-metal QA checks (tonal range, edge separation, no artificial reflection geometry) and the dial-legibility check (markers/hands not obscured by crystal glare).
4. If any single element fails (e.g., date window appears, hands wrong style, crystal glare obscures markers) re-generate/inpaint only that region rather than accepting a full-image redo, to preserve everything else that already matches spec.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Final color grade: deterministic LUT/curve pass to lock in a consistent cool-neutral background and true dial blue (matching brand color standard across the catalog), applied in standard post rather than left to generation variance.
- Safe-margin/crop guide: fixed top margin (~15% of frame height) reserved for page headline/price overlay per template — enforced by deterministic crop, not by the generator.
- Dust/spec retouch and micro-scratch cleanup on any rendered case/crystal artifacts: manual retouch pass, not generative.
- Optional deterministic drop-shadow/ground-contact refinement to match the page's shadow style used across the product catalog for visual consistency.
- No logo, no watermark, no on-image text — those remain page-template elements outside the image asset.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable (static hero image, no motion/audio). Edit pipeline consists solely of the deterministic post steps above (grade, crop/margin, retouch) applied after the single generated frame is approved against the QA checklist.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Date window reappearing on the dial (common generative default) → explicitly excluded in both positive framing ("no date window") and negative constraints; verify by inspection at 3 and 6 o'clock positions before acceptance.
- Hands rendered as generic straight batons instead of dauphine (faceted, tapered) → explicitly specified and checked.
- Crystal glare or a hard rectangular light-source reflection sitting over the dial center, killing legibility → controlled via geometry (arc near edge only) and flagged as a rejection condition.
- Steel case rendering as flat mid-grey (loses "brushed steel" credibility) or as blown-white/plastic-looking → checked against the tonal-range QA rule; regenerate lighting pass if it fails.
- Case/dial proportions drifting (oversized case, wrong marker count/spacing, asymmetric hands, extra complications) → locked via explicit geometry/count language and rejected on inspection.
- Drift toward generic "floating product on white" CGI render look → explicitly excluded via grounded tabletop/shadow/background staging and "no CGI sheen" negative constraint.
- Strap looking synthetic/rubbery instead of leather → grain, stitching and natural curl specified, checked visually.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: specified. ✔
- 38 mm brushed stainless-steel case: specified, with brushed-grain rendering explicitly engineered via lighting. ✔
- Deep blue sunburst dial: specified, with directional sunburst brushing made visible via key light angle. ✔
- Silver baton hour markers: specified. ✔
- Silver dauphine hands: specified. ✔
- No date window: explicitly required and explicitly negative-constrained. ✔
- Domed sapphire crystal: specified, with dome cue via edge highlight, kept off dial center for legibility. ✔
- Dark brown leather strap: specified, with grain/stitching. ✔
- Product geometry/details not casually altered: identity-lock elements repeated in prompt, QA checklist enforces before acceptance. ✔
- Avoid generic floating-product CGI unless creative idea genuinely requires it: creative idea is a grounded tabletop studio photograph, explicitly not floating/CGI-styled; only one concept produced. ✔
- Single concept only: one deliverable, one prompt. ✔
- No external website used: confirmed, none consulted. ✔

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) still image, 4:5 vertical aspect ratio (e.g. 3200×4000 px minimum for e-commerce zoom-in), for use as the primary hero image on a premium product detail page. Single final composited/generated frame — no alternates.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer needs a commercially deployable hero image for a fictional premium mechanical watch, the Aster Meridian 38, that must simultaneously (a) show the entire product accurately enough to serve as the primary purchase-decision image, (b) read as "premium/craftsmanship" through material and light rendering rather than through graphic device, and (c) hold every stated product fact rigidly — case size/finish, dial color and finish, marker/hand style, absence of date window, crystal profile, strap material and color. The brief explicitly rejects the generic "floating watch on white void" CGI cliché unless the concept demands it — it doesn't here, since a grounded, photographically-lit studio shot communicates material truth and desirability more credibly than a floating render.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A single, still-life studio product shot: the Aster Meridian 38 rests at a confident three-quarter angle on a dark, softly grained walnut surface, photographed as if with a macro-capable lens under classic tabletop product lighting. The watch is not floating, not on white seamless, not styled with props competing for attention — the product is the hero (a deliberate, simple presentation choice rather than a "creative" gimmick), and craftsmanship is communicated the way real premium watch photography communicates it: through controlled reflection behavior on the brushed case, a clean single highlight arc on the domed sapphire crystal that does not obscure the dial, and directional light that reveals the dial's radial sunburst brushing.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary read (first 1 second): "premium mechanical watch, whole object, unmistakably well made" — silhouette, case-to-dial-to-strap relationship, sunburst blue color.
2. Secondary read: dial craftsmanship — baton markers, dauphine hands, sunburst texture, clean absence of date window (a deliberate quiet-dial signal of design discipline).
3. Tertiary read: material cues — brushed steel grain on the case, dome curvature and clarity of the sapphire crystal, grain and stitching-free simplicity of the leather strap.
No text, logo, or price is placed in the image itself; this is a pure product hero image for a template that will carry a separate text layer.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Angle: elevated three-quarter view, dial tilted ~15–20° toward camera so the crystal dome's curvature and all hour markers remain legible without foreshortening a marker into invisibility.
- Framing: watch occupies roughly 65–70% of frame width, positioned slightly off-center (right-of-center) per a rule-of-thirds placement, strap trailing toward the lower-left foreground with a soft natural loop for depth.
- Surface: dark walnut wood, fine visible grain, matte-satin sheen (not glossy) — gives warmth and premium context without competing with the steel/blue palette.
- Background: seamless graduated charcoal-to-near-black backdrop, softly darkening toward the edges (subtle vignette) to keep all visual weight on the product.
- Focus: full end-to-end sharpness across the watch (case, dial, crystal surface, near strap coils) — appropriate for e-commerce accuracy — with only the far background/surface falling off very slightly softer.
- Color palette: deep sunburst blue (dial) + brushed steel neutral grey (case) + dark umber brown (strap) + warm dark wood + near-black background. No competing color accents.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Lighting (grounded in classic metal/product-lighting logic, applied cautiously since source is HOLD-status but directionally sound):
- Key: one large soft source (large softbox equivalent) camera-left/above, sized and positioned so its reflection family covers the brushed case at a controlled mid-grey-to-bright register — bright enough to read as polished/brushed steel, not blown to a flat white card.
- Fill: broad, low-contrast fill camera-right to open shadow detail on the case's far side and the strap, keeping the tonal spread narrow enough that detail survives on all visible faces of the product (a product with detail on every visible side needs a narrower highlight-to-shadow range than a plain geometric object).
- Edge/definition: a soft dark gobo positioned to place one controlled dark reflective arc along the case bezel and crystal edge, giving the metal a visible "edge" against the light background rather than letting it merge into a flat gradient.
- Crystal: light angled so the sapphire dome shows one soft, curved specular highlight in the upper third of the dial only — never centered over the markers or hands, so the sunburst texture and all indices stay fully legible beneath it.
- Dial: light raked slightly off-axis to catch the sunburst dial's radial brushing so the texture is visible as texture, not just as flat blue.
- Strap: soft, slightly lower-key light so the dark brown leather reads with visible grain rather than going to solid black.
- Camera/lens equivalent: macro-capable ~90–100mm equivalent, narrow aperture equivalent (f/8–f/11 look) for full product sharpness, tripod-locked framing.
- Color grade: neutral premium grade — protect true blue-grey neutrality in the steel (no color cast), keep the dial blue rich but not oversaturated, warm the wood slightly, crush background toward near-black without clipping to pure 0.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final image prompt (one shot, one deliverable):

"Premium studio product photograph of a men's luxury mechanical wristwatch, three-quarter elevated angle, dial tilted toward camera, resting on a dark satin walnut wood surface against a seamless graduated charcoal-to-black studio backdrop. The watch: 38mm brushed stainless-steel round case with visible fine linear brushing texture, deep blue sunburst dial showing radial brushed-metal texture under raking light, thin silver baton hour markers evenly spaced, slim silver dauphine hour and minute hands only (no seconds hand, no sub-dials, no date window or date aperture anywhere on the dial), a clear domed sapphire crystal with one soft curved specular highlight in the upper third of the dial that does not obscure any marker or hand, dark brown leather strap with natural grain trailing in a soft loop toward the lower foreground, slightly out of hard focus at the very tip only. Full edge-to-edge sharpness across case, dial and near strap. Soft directional key light from upper left creating a controlled bright register on the brushed case without blowing out to flat white, gentle fill from the right preserving shadow-side detail, one soft dark reflection arc along the case bezel for dimensional definition, soft contact shadow beneath the case on the wood surface. No text, no logo, no wordmark, no brand name printed anywhere on the dial or case. No floating product, no pure white background, no CGI-render look — photographic tabletop studio realism, premium editorial product photography aesthetic. Vertical 4:5 composition, watch occupying roughly two-thirds of frame width, positioned right-of-center."

Negative/exclusion guidance (to be paired with the prompt): no date window, no seconds hand, no chronograph sub-dials, no extra hour markers beyond evenly spaced batons, no printed text/logo/numerals on dial, no mirror-chrome/blown-white case, no blown-out crystal highlight covering hands or markers, no warped/elliptical case distortion, no visible crew/studio/camera reflection in crystal or bezel, no floating-in-void background, no color cast shifting steel toward gold/yellow, no strap color drifting to black or tan.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
None required for this deliverable — no logo/text overlay, no deterministic compositing layer, since the brief calls for a single clean product hero image with no on-image copy. Production note: if a physical prototype of the Aster Meridian 38 ever exists, the strongly preferred production path for absolute geometric/material fidelity is real macro photography using this same lighting recipe, with the generated image retained only as a pre-visualization/concept reference — generation is used here specifically because no physical reference exists for this fictional product.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static image deliverable.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Dial over-complication: generation must not invent a seconds hand, sub-dial, date window, or brand wordmark not specified by the customer — re-check final frame against the fixed fact list before sign-off.
- Crystal blowout: verify the specular highlight on the dome does not cross over any hour marker or hand; if it does, regenerate/retouch to relocate the highlight.
- Case geometry distortion: verify the case reads as a true circle from the chosen angle (elliptical compression is acceptable only as much as the tilt angle justifies) and that brushing texture direction is consistent across the case, not patchy or mismatched between lugs.
- Flat/lifeless metal: if the case reads as flat mid-grey with no highlight structure, the "family of angles" light placement has failed — increase key light size/proximity until a controlled bright register appears, without clipping to white.
- Dial legibility loss: if sunburst texture or markers disappear into overexposed blue or underexposed shadow, narrow the lighting ratio — a product with detail on every visible face needs a narrower tonal range than a plain object.
- Strap color drift: confirm strap renders as dark brown with visible grain, not black or auburn/tan.
- Background competing for attention: confirm backdrop stays a smooth, low-detail gradient with no texture or object that could split attention from the product.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK

- Dark brown leather strap: specified. ✔
- 4:5 aspect ratio: specified. ✔

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One static hero image, 4:5 vertical (portrait), for premium e-commerce product-detail page use. Single final frame, no variants.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer needs a commercially deployable hero shot that functions as the primary purchase-decision image on a watch product page. It must (a) prove the specified geometry and materials are exactly as stated — nothing invented or substituted, (b) let a shopper read dial layout, hand shapes, marker finish and case brushing at a glance, and (c) feel expensive: controlled reflections, disciplined tonal range, no plasticky CGI sheen. "Desirability" here is delivered through optical credibility (it looks like a real, well-made object photographed well) rather than through mood, story or lifestyle staging — the product itself is the entire creative content.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A single studio "product-portrait" of the Aster Meridian 38, three-quarter angle, isolated against a quiet dark-to-charcoal gradient so the brushed steel, the sunburst dial and the domed crystal each get their own controlled highlight while the leather strap grounds the bottom of the frame. No hands, no wrist, no lifestyle set — the watch is deliberately made the hero, photographed with the discipline of real macro product photography rather than rendered as a floating generic CGI object. Craftsmanship is communicated by three deliberately engineered light events: a directional sweep across the sunburst dial, a controlled arc highlight on the domed sapphire, and a grazing highlight that reveals the brushed grain of the case — each one is optical evidence, not a texture filter.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary read (first 0.5s): the watch as a complete, desirable object — silhouette, proportion, blue dial glow against dark ground.
2. Secondary read: dial detail — sunburst brushing catching light, silver baton markers and dauphine hands crisply legible, clean dial with no date window (confirms product fact by omission).
3. Tertiary read: material cues — brushed case highlight, domed crystal's refractive edge, leather strap grain and stitching.
4. Supporting: overall commercial cleanliness — enough negative space at top/bottom for platform UI overlays (price, CTA) without crowding the product.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Framing: 4:5 vertical, watch angled at roughly 30–35° horizontal rotation (crown-side visible, three-quarter dial view), slight downward camera pitch (~15°) so the dial sunburst and crystal dome both catch light without the lens reflecting square into the crystal.
- Background: seamless gradient from deep charcoal-grey (top) to near-black (bottom edge), warm-neutral, no props, no logos, no text.
- Surface: watch resting on a matte dark riser/pedestal edge just entering bottom of frame, strap coiling gently forward toward camera to fill the lower third and lead the eye up to the dial — this is what fills the 4:5 vertical format instead of empty CGI void.
- Palette: near-black/charcoal background, brushed cool-grey steel, deep blue dial, warm dark-brown leather — one warm accent (leather) against a cool-dominant frame so the product visually separates from its own background.
- Lens/optics logic: long-ish "macro" focal length equivalent (~100mm) with minimal perspective distortion so case roundness and lug geometry are not bent; depth of field moderate — full case, dial and crystal in acceptably sharp focus, with only the trailing strap and far background edge softening off, enough to direct the eye without hiding the fine dial detail an e-commerce buyer needs to see.
- Reflection control: crystal and case must show one clean, shaped highlight sweep each (not multiple chaotic hotspots, not a mirrored studio reflection of equipment). No visible camera or rig reflection in the crystal or case.
- Tonal discipline: steel highlight kept a controlled light-grey, not blown to pure white; darkest shadow on case kept a dark grey, not crushed to black — so brushing texture, bezel edge and lug facets stay legible throughout (a full-page e-commerce hero must keep detail on every visible surface, not just at one extreme).

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Type: still image, single frame, generated then lightly art-directed/graded.
Construction logic (translatable to real studio physics for reference/QC even though this is generative):
1. Key light: one large soft source positioned so only the portion of it that falls inside the case's/crystal's reflective "family of angles" (relative to camera) lights those surfaces brightly; the same source's broader spread lights the background and strap by soft diffuse falloff. This is what produces a case/dial that reads bright and dimensional while the background stays a controlled, separately-toned gradient — one light logic, two independent tonal outcomes.
2. Fill: soft, large, low-contrast fill opposite key to hold shadow-side case detail open (avoid black clipping on the far lugs/bezel).
3. Dial accent: a narrow secondary highlight or the key's grazing edge is aimed to sweep across the sunburst brushing at an angle that makes the radial brushing visible as a light-to-dark gradient across the dial face — this is the primary "craftsmanship" cue for a sunburst dial and must not be flat, shadowless fill.
4. Crystal: domed sapphire shows one soft, elongated highlight arc near its upper edge (proof of dome curvature) without obscuring hour markers or hands.
5. Case: brushed steel shows a directional highlight running along the brushing grain on the bezel/lug tops, distinguishing "brushed" from "polished" — critical since the product fact specifies brushed, not polished, steel.
6. Strap: soft, closer, slightly warmer fill to bring out grain and stitching texture without competing with the dial as the brightest point in the frame.
7. Camera angle chosen specifically off-square to the flattest crystal/case planes so no direct specular return of light source or rig appears center-frame — angle plus long focal length keeps geometry true while avoiding a dead mirror hotspot.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single final image prompt (execute as one generation, 4:5 aspect ratio):

"Premium studio product photograph of a men's mechanical wristwatch, three-quarter angle beauty shot, shot on a macro-capable ~100mm lens with natural, undistorted proportions. The watch: 38mm brushed stainless-steel round case with visible directional brushing catching a controlled grazing highlight along its grain on the bezel and lug tops; deep blue sunburst dial with a visible radial brushed-light sweep across its face going from a brighter arc to a darker arc; silver baton hour markers and silver dauphine hands, crisp and fully legible, no date window anywhere on the dial; domed sapphire crystal with one soft elongated highlight arc near the upper rim proving its curvature, no reflection of camera or studio equipment visible in the crystal or case; dark brown leather strap with visible grain and stitching, coiling gently toward camera and filling the lower third of the frame, resting on a dark matte riser edge. Background is a smooth seamless gradient from deep charcoal grey at top to near-black at the bottom edge, no props, no text, no logos, no hands or wrist. Lighting: large soft key producing a bright but non-clipped highlight on the steel and dial, gentle fill preventing shadow-side detail loss on the case, warm soft accent light on the strap. Full case and dial in sharp focus with a slight, natural falloff toward the trailing strap and far background only. Color palette: cool brushed steel, deep saturated blue dial, warm dark brown leather, neutral charcoal background. Mood: precise, expensive, quiet, commercial hero product photography, catalog-grade sharpness and clarity, no CGI floating look, no glossy toy-like plastic sheen, photorealistic material rendering. Vertical 4:5 composition with clean negative space above and below the watch for e-commerce page overlays."

CONSTRUCTION_PLAN (for the single image)
- Step 1: Establish composition — three-quarter dial-forward angle, ~15° downward camera pitch, watch occupying central-upper two-thirds of frame, strap flowing into lower third.
- Step 2: Lock product-fact invariants into the prompt explicitly (size cue via proportion, brushed steel, blue sunburst dial, silver baton markers, silver dauphine hands, no date window, domed sapphire, dark brown leather) so no attribute drifts.
- Step 3: Encode the three highlight events (dial sweep, crystal arc, brushed-grain highlight) as the craftsmanship payload.
- Step 4: Encode background/negative-space requirement for page-overlay usability.
- Step 5: Post-generation QC pass (see FAILURE_PREVENTION) before delivery.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Final crop/safe-margin check for 4:5 and for e-commerce UI overlay zones is a deterministic post-process step (not part of generation): confirm ~8–10% clear margin top and bottom.
- Optional deterministic color-grade pass (fixed LUT/curve) to standardize dial blue and leather brown against the brand's existing product-page palette, applied uniformly, not generative.
- No compositing of separately generated elements is required; this is a single-shot construction.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static still image deliverable.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Geometry drift: verify case is round (not ovalized by lens distortion), lugs symmetrical, crown present and correctly placed at 3 o'clock — reject/regenerate if case proportions or dial layout skew.
- Attribute drift checklist before approval: brushed (not polished/mirror) steel finish visible as directional grain, not mirror gloss; dial is sunburst blue, not flat/navy/black; markers and hands are silver baton/dauphine, not gold, not applied-logo, not numeral; confirm no date window is rendered; crystal is visibly domed (has curvature highlight), not flat; strap is dark brown leather with visible grain, not black, not metal bracelet.
- Reflection failure: reject if camera rig, studio softbox shape, or any recognizable equipment silhouette appears mirrored in crystal or case — this is the most common "generic CGI" tell.
- Clipping failure: reject if dial or steel highlights blow to pure white with no texture, or shadows crush to pure black losing bezel/lug definition — detail must survive on every visible surface, per product-page legibility requirement.
- Genericness failure: reject renders that look like a floating, shadowless, materially ambiguous CGI product with no grounding surface/shadow contact — the watch must show a real contact shadow and surface interaction with its riser.
- Focus failure: reject if the dial's hour markers or hand shapes are not crisply legible at typical product-page zoom; this is a specification detail, not a mood detail, and cannot be softened away by shallow depth of field.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 aspect ratio: specified in prompt and crop plan. ✔
- 38mm brushed stainless-steel case: explicit in prompt with brushing-highlight construction. ✔
- Deep blue sunburst dial: explicit, with radial-highlight mechanism to prove sunburst finish. ✔
- Silver baton hour markers, silver dauphine hands: explicit and required legible. ✔
- No date window: explicit negative constraint stated in prompt. ✔
- Domed sapphire crystal: explicit, with curvature-proving highlight arc. ✔
- Dark brown leather strap: explicit, with grain/stitching requirement. ✔
- No casual alteration of product geometry/details: addressed via lens choice (minimal distortion), explicit attribute lock, and QC checklist. ✔
- Avoid generic floating CGI unless idea requires it: idea explicitly rejects floating CGI in favor of grounded studio product photography with real contact shadow. ✔
- No external website: none consulted, none referenced. ✔
- Single concept only: one image, one construction plan, no alternates presented. ✔

Apply the Stage-1 image rubric. Choose exactly one A/B/C winner.

1. Dial Detail (Deep blue sunburst + silver markers) -> Primary focus.
2. Material Quality (Brushed steel + brown leather) -> Supporting evidence of luxury.
3. Product Form (38mm proportions + domed crystal) -> Establishing the silhouette.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
*   Color Palette: Deep Navy, Silver/Steel, Dark Brown, Charcoal Grey.
*   Lighting: Dual-source studio lighting. One large softbox from the top-left to create a soft gradient across the dial; one rim light from the right to define the brushed edge of the case and the curvature of the crystal.
*   Composition: 4:5 Aspect Ratio. Watch positioned slightly off-center to the right, creating negative space on the left for potential marketing copy. Shallow depth of field (f/5.6 - f/8) to keep the dial in sharp focus while softly blurring the strap trailing off-frame.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1.  Set background to dark charcoal matte stone to avoid distracting reflections.
2.  Position the watch at a 45-degree tilt, face slightly angled toward the viewer.
3.  Apply a linear light source to trigger the "sunburst" effect on the dial.
4.  Ensure the "brushed" texture of the stainless steel is visible via specular highlights along the lugs.
5.  Render with high-fidelity macro optics to ensure the dauphine hands and baton markers have crisp, clean edges.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Professional macro e-commerce photography of a luxury mechanical wristwatch, the Aster Meridian 38. 4:5 aspect ratio. The watch features a 38mm brushed stainless-steel case, a deep blue sunburst dial with silver baton hour markers and silver dauphine hands, no date window, and a prominent domed sapphire crystal. The watch is attached to a dark brown leather strap with visible fine grain. The watch is resting at a 45-degree angle on a dark matte charcoal slate surface. Studio lighting: soft top-down key light creating a radial sunburst glow on the dial, and a sharp rim light highlighting the brushed metal texture of the case and the curvature of the glass. Hyper-realistic, 8k resolution, sharp focus on the dial, shallow depth of field, luxury watch magazine aesthetic, neutral color balance, clean composition.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
*   Case: 38mm / Brushed Stainless Steel (Non-negotiable).
*   Dial: Deep Blue Sunburst / No Date (Non-negotiable).
*   Hands/Markers: Silver Dauphine / Silver Baton (Non-negotiable).
*   Crystal: Domed Sapphire (Non-negotiable).
*   Strap: Dark Brown Leather (Non-negotiable).

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
N/A (Static Image).

FAILURE_PREVENTION
*   Constraint: "No date window." -> Prompt explicitly states "no date window" to prevent AI from adding a default date complication.
*   Constraint: "Brushed stainless-steel." -> Prompt specifies "brushed metal texture" and "specular highlights" to avoid a generic polished/chrome look.
*   Constraint: "Avoid generic floating CGI." -> Watch is grounded on a physical slate surface.
*   Constraint: "Product geometry." -> Specified as "38mm" and "dauphine hands" to ensure the classic proportions are maintained.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
*   4:5 Ratio? Yes.
*   Brushed steel? Yes.
*   Deep blue sunburst dial? Yes.
*   Silver baton markers/dauphine hands? Yes.
*   No date window? Yes.
*   Domed sapphire? Yes.
*   Dark brown leather strap? Yes.
*   No generic floating CGI? Yes.
*   No external website? Yes.

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One 4:5 premium promotional poster.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The objective is to communicate a highly competitive price point (₹9 for images, ₹99 for video) to Indian B2B customers during the festive season without compromising the brand's identity as a serious, reliable AI infrastructure provider. The "premium" feel is achieved by avoiding retail tropes (starbursts, loud reds, cluttered text) and instead using a high-contrast, minimalist "Dark Mode" aesthetic with luxury festive accents.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Precision Festive." The design pairs the mathematical rigor of an API (grid systems, dark mode, clean typography) with the warmth and prestige of Indian festivities (deep gold and saffron light). The price points are treated as "architectural elements" rather than "discounts," making them feel like standard, efficient infrastructure costs.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. THE VALUE (Focal Point): ₹9 / ₹99 (Massive, bold, immediate).
2. THE PRODUCT (Context): Image Generation / Video Generation.
3. THE TARGET/OCCASION (Secondary): Festive Edition for Indian Businesses.
4. THE TRUST SIGNALS (Tertiary): One domestic GST invoice. No forex fees.
5. BRANDING (Anchor): Aight logo and getaight.ai.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: Obsidian Black (#0B0B0B) background; Champagne Gold (#D4AF37) and Saffron (#F4C430) for light accents; Crisp White (#FFFFFF) for primary typography.
- Typography: High-contrast geometric sans-serif (e.g., Inter or Montserrat).
    - Prices: Ultra-bold, heavy weight, oversized.
    - Labels: Medium weight, all-caps, wide tracking (letter-spacing).
    - Details: Light weight, clean, centered.
- Composition: Centered, symmetrical grid. Significant negative space to ensure a "gallery" or "infrastructure" feel.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. GENERATE BACKGROUND: Create a high-fidelity abstract 3D render representing "generative AI" using a luxury festive palette.
2. TYPOGRAPHIC OVERLAY:
    - Place the Aight logo at the top center.
    - Create a two-column grid in the center.
    - Left Column: "IMAGE" (small, white, tracked) above "₹9" (massive, gold).
    - Right Column: "VIDEO" (small, white, tracked) above "₹99" (massive, gold).
    - Below the grid: "Festive Edition for Indian Businesses" (medium white).
    - Bottom anchor: "One domestic GST invoice. No forex fees." (light grey/white).
    - Footer: "getaight.ai" (medium white).

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Final Generation Prompt (Background):
"A high-end cinematic 3D abstract render for a luxury tech brand. Deep obsidian black background. Intricate, glowing filaments of liquid gold and saffron silk weaving into a precise geometric grid and circuit patterns. Hyper-realistic, 8k resolution, architectural lighting, soft bokeh, minimalist composition with vast negative space in the center and top for typography. The mood is sophisticated, prestigious, and futuristic. --ar 4:5"

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Typography: All text must be rendered via design software (not AI) to ensure precision of the rupee symbol (₹) and exact pricing.
- Logo: Official Aight logo.
- Layout: Strict 4:5 centered grid.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
N/A (Static Image).

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Avoid: Traditional "Sale" or "Offer" banners.
- Avoid: Using generic "Diya" or "Firecracker" clip art which cheapens the B2B infrastructure positioning.
- Avoid: Bright red or yellow backgrounds that mimic retail flyers.
- Ensure: The rupee symbol is correctly rendered and prominent.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Premium feel? Yes (Obsidian/Gold palette, minimal layout).
- B2B Infrastructure vibe? Yes (Grid system, focus on GST/Forex).
- Price immediately understandable? Yes (Massive center-weighted typography).
- 4:5 Aspect Ratio? Yes.
- Used only getaight.ai info? Yes (GST/Forex/Rupee wallet focus).

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One 4:5 promotional poster.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The goal is to communicate a high-value price point (₹9 for images, ₹99 for videos) to Indian businesses during the festive season without sacrificing the brand's identity as a serious, professional AI infrastructure provider. The design must avoid "discount" visual tropes (loud colors, starbursts, cluttered layouts) and instead use the language of premium tech branding: minimalism, rigorous typography, and intentional negative space.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Festive Ledger." The design treats the pricing not as a "sale," but as a transparent, fixed cost of infrastructure. By using a structured, grid-based layout reminiscent of a technical specification sheet or a high-end architectural portfolio, the "discount" prices are elevated to "efficient units of production."

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary: The Prices (₹9 | ₹99) — The core commercial hook.
2. Secondary: The Assets (IMAGE | VIDEO) — Defining what is being bought.
3. Tertiary: The Framing (Scale your festive presence) — Contextualizing the offer for the season.
4. Foundation: Brand Identity (Aight logo + "Domestic GST. Prepaid Rupees.") — Establishing trust and infrastructure legitimacy.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Palette: Deep Charcoal (#1A1A1A) background with stark White (#FFFFFF) typography. A single accent color: Metallic Champagne Gold (#D4AF37) used exclusively for a thin, technical ornament.
- Typography:
    - Prices: Extra-bold, oversized sans-serif (Swiss style) for maximum impact.
    - Labels: Monospace font (e.g., JetBrains Mono or Roboto Mono) to signal "API/Developer/Infrastructure."
    - Body/Header: Clean, medium-weight sans-serif.
- Imagery: No stock photos. The "festive" element is a single, sophisticated gold-wireframe geometric motif in the corner—a hybrid between a traditional mandala and a neural network graph—rendered in a 1pt line weight.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Layout: Use a strict 4:5 vertical grid.
2. Header: Place "Scale your festive presence" at the top left, small and understated.
3. Centerpiece: Divide the center into two equal vertical columns.
    - Left Column: Large "₹9" centered, with "IMAGE" in monospace underneath.
    - Right Column: Large "₹99" centered, with "VIDEO" in monospace underneath.
    - A thin vertical gold line separates the two columns.
4. Footer: The Aight logo at the bottom left. To the right, a clean line of text: "Prepaid Rupees. Domestic GST. Serious AI."
5. Ornament: Place the gold technical mandala in the top right or bottom right, partially bleeding off the edge to create a modern, asymmetrical feel.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
(Since this is a typography-heavy, layout-specific poster, the prompt focuses on the "Swiss Tech" aesthetic and the specific spatial arrangement.)

"A premium, ultra-minimalist 4:5 promotional poster for an AI company. Background is a solid, matte deep charcoal. The composition is governed by a strict Swiss design grid. In the center, two massive, bold white numbers: '₹9' and '₹99', separated by a thin, elegant vertical champagne-gold line. Below '₹9' is the word 'IMAGE' in a small, white monospace technical font. Below '₹99' is the word 'VIDEO' in the same monospace font. At the top left, the phrase 'Scale your festive presence' in a clean, small sans-serif white font. In the bottom corner, a highly sophisticated, thin-line gold wireframe geometric ornament that looks like a fusion of a mandala and a circuit board. High contrast, luxury tech aesthetic, zero clutter, wide margins, architectural precision, 8k resolution, graphic design masterpiece."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Precise text placement (Header, Footer) should be handled via layout software to ensure the "infrastructure" feel isn't ruined by generative text artifacts.
- The Aight logo must be placed as a vector overlay.
- The specific "Domestic GST" tagline must be rendered in a precise monospace font.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
N/A (Static Image).

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Avoid: Any imagery of fireworks, sweets, or traditional clothing, which would trigger the "discount flyer" feel.
- Avoid: Using red or bright yellow, which are associated with retail sales.
- Avoid: Centering the text in a way that feels like a greeting card; keep it aligned to a technical grid.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 ratio? Yes.
- ₹9 and ₹99 visible? Yes.
- Serious AI infra vibe? Yes (via Swiss minimalism and monospace type).
- Only used getaight.ai info? Yes (GST and Prepaid Rupee value props included).
- No discount flyer tropes? Yes.

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
Single premium advertising image, 4:5 aspect ratio.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
Create a high-end visual for a Mosambi sparkling drink targeting urban Millennials/Gen Z Indians. The image must pivot away from traditional "ethnic" tropes toward a "global-modern" aesthetic. Success is defined by the product looking like a sophisticated beverage (similar to a premium tonic or craft soda) rather than a mass-market soft drink, while remaining instantly recognizable as Mosambi-based.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"Minimalist Effervescence." A high-contrast, architectural still-life composition focusing on the interplay of light, cold glass, and the specific pale-yellow hue of mosambi. The image uses a muted, contemporary color palette and a "quiet luxury" approach to signify premium positioning.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. The Product (Hero): The bottle/glass as the central focal point.
2. Refreshment: Visual cues of extreme cold (condensation) and carbonation (bubbles).
3. Ingredient: Subtle, sophisticated presence of fresh mosambi.
4. Vibe: Sophisticated, urban, clean.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Color Palette: Pale Citrine (liquid), Muted Sage or Warm Slate (background), Crisp White (highlights).
- Lighting: Soft, directional side-lighting (simulating late afternoon urban window light) to create depth and accentuate textures.
- Composition: Center-weighted, slightly low-angle shot to give the product stature. Minimalist environment.
- Texture: Matte background vs. reflective glass and wet condensation.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
- Subject: A sleek, unlabeled premium glass bottle containing a pale-yellow sparkling liquid.
- Setting: A clean, matte-finished stone or concrete plinth in a neutral muted tone.
- Accents: A single, perfectly sliced translucent wheel of mosambi resting against the base of the bottle or floating subtly in a secondary highball glass beside it.
- Atmospheric Effects: Fine, tight carbonation bubbles clinging to the glass; a heavy layer of micro-condensation (dew) on the exterior.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Hyper-realistic commercial product photography, 4:5 aspect ratio. A premium, sleek glass bottle of pale-yellow sparkling mosambi drink as the central hero. The bottle is covered in crisp, cold condensation droplets. The liquid inside is effervescent with tiny, sophisticated carbonation bubbles. The bottle sits on a minimalist, matte-finished warm slate grey stone pedestal. Beside the bottle, a single, thin, translucent slice of fresh sweet lime (mosambi) rests elegantly. Lighting is soft, cinematic side-lighting with deep soft shadows, creating a high-end architectural feel. Background is a seamless, blurred neutral studio wall in a muted sage tone. No labels, no text, no people. Ultra-sharp focus on the glass texture and droplets. 8k resolution, shot on 85mm lens, f/2.8. Avoid any traditional Indian patterns, fabrics, or clichés.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
None.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
N/A (Static Image).

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- To avoid "cheap soft drink" look: Prohibit plastic textures, neon colors, and exaggerated "splash" effects.
- To avoid "clichéd Indian" look: Prohibit paisley, marigolds, traditional fabrics, and ornate gold carvings.
- To ensure "Product Hero": Use a shallow depth of field to blur the background and keep the bottle in razor-sharp focus.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 Aspect Ratio: Yes.
- Mosambi/Sparkling: Yes.
- Urban 22-35 target: Yes (via minimalist/modern aesthetic).
- No clichéd Indian shorthand: Yes.
- Not a cheap soft drink: Yes (via glass, lighting, and muted palette).
- Product as hero: Yes.
- No celebrity: Yes.
- No external website: Yes.

# DIAGNOSTIC EXTRA

## ID01 — B02

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) static promotional poster, 4:5 aspect ratio (e.g., 1080×1350 px, scalable vector-safe), for paid social/web placement and possibly print. Single final image. No variants.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer needs one poster that does two things simultaneously without contradiction: (1) make ₹9 (image) and ₹99 (video) instantly legible and unambiguous — this is the commercial payload and the reason the poster exists; (2) make the brand read as serious AI infrastructure for businesses, not a discount retail flyer, during a festive season context. Per getaight.ai, Aight's actual brand voice is plain, declarative, numeric, and merchant/ledger-coded ("the counter is open," "prepaid rupees," "one domestic GST invoice," "hard caps") — it already talks about itself as a counter/ledger, not a lifestyle brand. That gives a legitimate, source-grounded route to festive without kitsch: treat the poster as a rate card / ledger entry, not a sale banner. Festiveness is expressed through a single restrained warm-gold accent and one small graphic mark, not through discount-flyer devices (bursts, slashes, confetti, "SALE," multiple colors). The brief explicitly asks that typography and hierarchy — not illustration — carry the work, which aligns with treating this as a precision rate-card object.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Festive Rate Card." The poster is designed as a premium, single-color-accented ledger/invoice line-item card: two price rows (IMAGE / VIDEO) rendered as oversized tabular numerals against labelled line items, framed by the language Aight already uses about itself (prepaid rupees, one GST invoice, no surprise bills). A festive season is acknowledged through one small line-drawn diya mark and a warm gold accent — nothing more. This makes the ₹9/₹99 proposition read like a fact on a merchant's counter (credible, infrastructural, exact) rather than a promotional discount (temporary, negotiable, loud).

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
Priority order (also the reading path, top to bottom, left-aligned ledger grid):
1. Occasion eyebrow (smallest, quietest, sets timing only): "FESTIVE SEASON RATE CARD"
2. Hero tier — the two prices, largest and heaviest elements on the page, each paired with an unambiguous plain-English label:
   a. IMAGE GENERATION — ₹9
   b. VIDEO GENERATION — ₹99
3. Trust/credibility line (mid-size, quieter than prices, louder than footer): "Prepaid rupees. One domestic GST invoice. No surprise bills."
4. Brand identity block (footer, smallest of the named elements but always present): "Aight ." wordmark + "Where Indian businesses buy AI." + getaight.ai
No other copy competes for attention. Nothing is ranked above the two prices. Hierarchy is built entirely from scale and weight contrast within one typeface family and a fixed left-alignment column grid — the two controls (size contrast, spacing/density contrast) that most reliably establish hierarchy in a layout — plus a strict column-width discipline that differentiates the "label" text from the "price" text so the eye never confuses the two roles.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Format: 4:5, portrait, generous fixed margins (~7% of width each side), everything left-aligned to one vertical grid; prices right-aligned to a second column so the eye reads label→price on a straight baseline, invoice-style.
- Palette: near-black charcoal ground (#121212–#161616), warm paper-white text (#F2EEE4 at full and ~70% opacity for secondary lines), one festive accent — muted antique/marigold gold (#C9A24A) used only for: the ₹ glyph in both prices, the thin rule under the eyebrow, the thin rules between the two price rows, and one small line-drawn diya mark. No other colors. No gradients, no photographic imagery, no confetti/burst/ribbon devices, no red "sale" treatment.
- Typography: a single grotesque/geometric sans family in three weights only (Light, Regular, Black/Bold with true tabular lining numerals). Labels and body set in Regular/Light with wide, even letterspacing on the eyebrow and footer for a "printed ledger" feel. The two prices set in the Black/Bold weight at dramatically larger size than everything else on the page (the single largest size jump on the poster, deliberately extreme, since the price is the reason the asset exists). Numerals must use tabular/lining figures so "₹9" and "₹99" align cleanly on their price column despite different digit counts.
- One small non-typographic mark: a minimal single-stroke gold line-drawing of a diya (a shallow open arc + a single flame teardrop, no shading, no photorealism) placed as a divider between the eyebrow and the first price row, or as a small glyph beside the section rule — the only "festive" iconography permitted, kept graphic and abstract, never cute or illustrative.
- Overall mood reference: a merchant's rate card, an invoice line item, a stock-ticker board — precise, tabular, unornamented, confident. Not: a retail sale poster, not a lifestyle/consumer app ad, not a rangoli/collage festive graphic.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Lock copy exactly as specified below (no paraphrase, no additional claims — see DETERMINISTIC section).
2. Build the layout in a vector/design tool (or via the generative background prompt below plus deterministic text) on a 1080×1350 canvas.
3. Establish the grid: left content column starting at 7% margin; label text and price text share one baseline per row; a second right-aligned column anchors the price numerals so ₹9 and ₹99 both terminate flush right.
4. Set the eyebrow line, thin gold rule, diya mark, two price rows with dividing gold rule between them, trust line, and footer wordmark block in that vertical order with generously increasing space "weight" between tiers (tightest spacing within a row, most open spacing between the price block and the footer) so that spatial rhythm itself reinforces which lines belong together and which are separate ideas.
5. Render at full resolution; verify numeral accuracy against source copy before export (see FAILURE_PREVENTION).

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Single executable prompt (background/texture + composition layer; exact copy to be set deterministically as described in the next section — see note below on why):

"A premium minimalist poster background, 4:5 portrait aspect ratio. Deep charcoal-black flat backdrop (#121212) with an extremely subtle fine paper/ledger-page grain texture, almost imperceptible, no visible photograph or scene. A thin horizontal warm antique-gold rule (#C9A24A) sits near the top third of the frame, with a small minimal single-line gold drawing of a diya (oil lamp) beside it — rendered as a simple open arc with one small teardrop flame, flat line art only, no shading, no gradient, no photorealism, no additional festive clutter. A second, matching thin gold horizontal rule sits roughly at the vertical center of the frame, dividing the composition into an upper and lower price zone. The overall composition is calm, exact, and spacious, with wide even margins on all four sides (roughly 7% of width), evoking a merchant's ledger or an invoice line-item card rather than a retail advertisement — no bursts, no confetti, no ribbons, no discount-sale graphics, no photographic products, no human figures, no additional colors beyond charcoal, warm paper-white, and antique gold. Leave large clear negative-space bands above the top rule, between the two rules, below the second rule, and at the bottom of the frame, sized to later receive large tabular numeral typography and short lines of text — do not render any text, numerals, or letterforms in the image itself. Mood: serious financial/infrastructure precision with one restrained festive accent, premium and quiet, not consumer or playful."

Deterministic text overlay (set in post-production typesetting, exact strings, one grotesque sans family, tabular figures):
- Eyebrow (small caps, wide tracking, paper-white 70%): "FESTIVE SEASON RATE CARD"
- Row 1 label (Regular, paper-white): "IMAGE GENERATION" — Row 1 price (Black weight, largest element on page, gold ₹ + paper-white numeral): "₹9"
- Row 2 label (Regular, paper-white): "VIDEO GENERATION" — Row 2 price (Black weight, same size as Row 1 price, gold ₹ + paper-white numeral): "₹99"
- Trust line (Regular, paper-white 80%, smaller than labels): "Prepaid rupees. One domestic GST invoice. No surprise bills."
- Footer wordmark (Regular/Light): "Aight ." on one line, "Where Indian businesses buy AI." directly beneath in smaller size, "getaight.ai" smallest, all paper-white at reduced opacity for quiet close.

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
All copy — the two prices (₹9, ₹99), both labels, the trust line, and the entire footer wordmark/tagline/URL block — must be set as deterministic, precisely controlled typesetting (vector text layer), not left to a generative model's text-rendering. Reasons: (a) price accuracy is a hard commercial constraint and a single misrendered digit is unacceptable; (b) tabular numeral alignment and exact letterspacing are required for the "ledger" effect and are unreliable in current text-in-image generation; (c) the brand wordmark "Aight ." and tagline are verbatim quotes from the only permitted source and must not be altered or reinterpreted. Only the background texture, the two gold rules, and the single diya line-mark may be produced generatively (or drawn directly in a vector tool, which is the lower-risk option and is recommended).

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
Not applicable — static single image deliverable, no motion or audio component.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Digit/price error: the single highest-severity risk. Mitigation: set price text deterministically, proofread against the brief twice, and never allow a generative model to render the numerals inside the image itself.
- Drift toward "discount flyer" look: mitigation — no burst/starburst shapes, no strikethrough "was/now" pricing, no red, no multiple accent colors, no exclamation marks, no "SALE"/"OFFER" language anywhere in copy.
- Over-decoration of the diya mark: keep it to one flat gold line-stroke; if it starts to look illustrative/cute in any draft, remove it — the poster must still work with zero iconography, since typography is the mandated carrier of hierarchy.
- Copy invention: do not add claims not present on getaight.ai (no fabricated stats, no invented model names, no claims about speed/quality not stated on the site). The trust line used is a close paraphrase of actual site language ("prepaid rupees," "one domestic GST invoice," "no surprise bills" — all present verbatim or near-verbatim on the site) and should not be embellished further.
- Legibility at small size (feed thumbnail): verify the two price numerals remain the single largest, most dominant elements even when the poster is scaled down to thumbnail size; if hierarchy collapses at small size, increase the size gap between price numerals and everything else rather than adding color or ornament.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 4:5 poster: ✔ specified.
- ₹9 image / ₹99 video, both immediately understandable: ✔ largest, boldest elements on the page, plainly labelled.
- Feels like a serious AI infrastructure company, not a discount retail flyer: ✔ ledger/rate-card system, single restrained gold accent, no discount-flyer devices, brand voice and claims sourced only from getaight.ai.
- Typography and information hierarchy do most of the work: ✔ single typeface family, weight/size/spacing contrast and a strict alignment grid are the entire hierarchy mechanism; only one small non-typographic mark exists.
- Only getaight.ai used for product/brand information: ✔ all claims, wordmark, and tagline are taken directly from the frozen site snapshot; no other source used for brand/product facts.
- Single production-ready solution, no alternatives presented: ✔.

### JUDGE ID01
Score /100 with the same image rubric. Mark OPTIONAL_DIAGNOSTIC_PRODUCTION only if >=80 and there is no material hard-constraint violation.
